EXECUTIVE READING

先给结论，再进入源码

核心机制

终端、桌面和服务端不是各写一套 Agent，而是都插到同一个“总电闸”上，所以权限、工具和生命周期不会因为换界面而变一套。

证据规模

31 条 finding / 30 处引用

主要边界

当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

适用建设

终端、桌面和服务端不是各写一套 Agent，而是都插到同一个“总电闸”上，所以权限、工具和生命周期不会因为换界面而变一套。

值得借鉴

  • 终端、桌面和服务端不是各写一套 Agent，而是都插到同一个“总电闸”上，所以权限、工具和生命周期不会因为换界面而变一套。
  • 它允许长任务一直做，但每个工具输出和“最后确认”都有保险丝；只要交付模式缺验收或验证，主机就不会让它假装完成。
  • 用户连按几次发送不会把同一个会话撕成两半：新消息要么排队，要么明确被拒；切换会话时也不会恰好换掉正在用的那份上下文。

需要警惕

  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

阅读方式

  • 先看固定提交，再看调用链
  • 事实、推断、风险分栏
  • 每条 finding 可回到源码
00 · METHOD

研究口径：不是 README 摘要

README POLICY

README 只定位入口和产品命名；实现结论必须回到运行时代码、契约或测试。

FACT POLICY

L1 运行代码优先，L2 类型/事件契约补足，L3/L4 交叉验证，L5 推断单独标记。

   90  func sessionsRecoveryCommand(args []string, cleanup bool) int {
   91  	name := "sessions diagnose"
   92  	if cleanup {
   93  		name = "sessions cleanup"
   94  	}
   95  	fs := flag.NewFlagSet(name, flag.ContinueOnError)
   96  	var dirs stringListFlag
   97  	apply := fs.Bool("apply", false, "move safe covered recovery branches to recoverable trash")
   98  	jsonOut := fs.Bool("json", false, "print the recovery report as JSON")
   99  	fs.Var(&dirs, "dir", "session directory to inspect; repeat for multiple directories")
  100  	if code, ok := parseCommandFlags(fs, args); !ok {
  101  		return code
  102  	}
  103  	if fs.NArg() != 0 || (!cleanup && *apply) {
  104  		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "usage: reasonix %s [--dir PATH] [--json]", name)
  105  		if cleanup {
  106  			fmt.Fprint(os.Stderr, " [--apply]")
  107  		}
  108  		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr)
  109  		return 2
  110  	}
  111  	if len(dirs) == 0 {
  112  		for _, target := range defaultSessionCatalogTargets() {
  113  			dirs = append(dirs, target.Path)
  114  		}
  115  	}
  116  	report := sessionRecoveryReport{Errors: []string{}, DryRun: !cleanup || !*apply}
  117  	seen := map[string]bool{}
  118  	for _, rawDir := range dirs {
  119  		dir := filepath.Clean(strings.TrimSpace(rawDir))
  120  		if dir == "." || dir == "" || seen[dir] {
  121  			continue
  122  		}
  123  		seen[dir] = true
  124  		report.Directories++
  125  		catalog, err := sessioncatalog.Open(context.Background(), sessioncatalog.Options{InMemory: true, DisableRepair: true})
  126  		if err != nil {
  127  			report.Errors = append(report.Errors, err.Error())
  128  			continue
  129  		}
  130  		err = catalog.ReconcileDirectory(context.Background(), sessioncatalog.DirectoryTarget{Path: dir, Scope: "global"})
  131  		if err != nil {
  132  			report.Errors = append(report.Errors, err.Error())
  133  			_ = catalog.Close(context.Background())
  134  			continue
  135  		}
  136  		groups, err := catalog.ListRecoveryGroups(context.Background(), dir)
  137  		if err != nil {
  138  			report.Errors = append(report.Errors, err.Error())
  139  			_ = catalog.Close(context.Background())
  140  			continue
  141  		}
  142  		for _, group := range groups {
  143  			rootID, members := group.ID, group.Members
  144  			report.Groups++
  145  			report.Branches += len(members)
  146  			canonical := ""
  147  			diverged := false
  148  			for _, member := range members {
  149  				if member.RecoveryCanonical && (member.RecoveryRole == sessioncatalog.RecoveryRoleAdopted || member.RecoveryRole == sessioncatalog.RecoveryRolePreferred) {
  150  					canonical = member.Path
  151  				}

   62  		{name: "/migrate", aliases: []string{"/migration"}, insert: "/migrate", hint: i18n.M.CmdMigrate, showInHelp: true},

   67  		{name: "/copy", insert: "/copy", hint: i18n.M.CmdCopy, showInHelp: true},

   72  func builtinSlashItems() []compItem {
   73  	specs := builtinSlashSpecs()
   74  	items := make([]compItem, 0, len(specs))
   75  	for _, spec := range specs {
   76  		items = append(items, compItem{
   77  			label: spec.name, insert: spec.insert, hint: spec.hint, descend: spec.descend,
   78  		})
   79  	}
   80  	return items
   81  }
   82
   83  func builtinSlashHelpItems() []compItem {
   84  	var items []compItem
   85  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   86  		if spec.showInHelp {
   87  			items = append(items, compItem{label: spec.name, hint: spec.hint})
   88  		}
   89  	}
   90  	return items
   91  }
   92
   93  func canonicalBuiltinSlashCommand(name string) string {
   94  	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
   95  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   96  		if name == spec.name {
   97  			return spec.name
   98  		}
   99  		if slices.Contains(spec.aliases, name) {
  100  			return spec.name
  101  		}
  102  	}
  103  	return name
  104  }

   95  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   96  		if name == spec.name {
   97  			return spec.name
   98  		}
   99  		if slices.Contains(spec.aliases, name) {
  100  			return spec.name
  101  		}
  102  	}
  103  	return name
  104  }

   72  func builtinSlashItems() []compItem {
   73  	specs := builtinSlashSpecs()
   74  	items := make([]compItem, 0, len(specs))
   75  	for _, spec := range specs {
   76  		items = append(items, compItem{
   77  			label: spec.name, insert: spec.insert, hint: spec.hint, descend: spec.descend,
   78  		})
   79  	}
   80  	return items
   81  }
   82
   83  func builtinSlashHelpItems() []compItem {
   84  	var items []compItem
   85  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   86  		if spec.showInHelp {
   87  			items = append(items, compItem{label: spec.name, hint: spec.hint})
   88  		}
   89  	}
   90  	return items
   91  }
   92
   93  func canonicalBuiltinSlashCommand(name string) string {
   94  	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
   95  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   96  		if name == spec.name {
   97  			return spec.name
   98  		}
   99  		if slices.Contains(spec.aliases, name) {
  100  			return spec.name
  101  		}
  102  	}
  103  	return name
  104  }

   72  func builtinSlashItems() []compItem {
   73  	specs := builtinSlashSpecs()
   74  	items := make([]compItem, 0, len(specs))
   75  	for _, spec := range specs {
   76  		items = append(items, compItem{
   77  			label: spec.name, insert: spec.insert, hint: spec.hint, descend: spec.descend,
   78  		})
   79  	}

   83  func builtinSlashHelpItems() []compItem {
   84  	var items []compItem
   85  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   86  		if spec.showInHelp {
   87  			items = append(items, compItem{label: spec.name, hint: spec.hint})
   88  		}
   89  	}
   90  	return items
   91  }
   92
   93  func canonicalBuiltinSlashCommand(name string) string {
   94  	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
   95  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   96  		if name == spec.name {
   97  			return spec.name
   98  		}
   99  		if slices.Contains(spec.aliases, name) {
  100  			return spec.name
  101  		}
  102  	}
  103  	return name
  104  }

   72  func builtinSlashItems() []compItem {
   73  	specs := builtinSlashSpecs()
   74  	items := make([]compItem, 0, len(specs))
   75  	for _, spec := range specs {
   76  		items = append(items, compItem{
   77  			label: spec.name, insert: spec.insert, hint: spec.hint, descend: spec.descend,
   78  		})
   79  	}
   80  	return items
   81  }
   82
   83  func builtinSlashHelpItems() []compItem {
   84  	var items []compItem
   85  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   86  		if spec.showInHelp {
   87  			items = append(items, compItem{label: spec.name, hint: spec.hint})
   88  		}
   89  	}
   90  	return items
   91  }
   92
   93  func canonicalBuiltinSlashCommand(name string) string {
   94  	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
   95  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   96  		if name == spec.name {
   97  			return spec.name
   98  		}
   99  		if slices.Contains(spec.aliases, name) {
  100  			return spec.name
  101  		}
  102  	}
  103  	return name
  104  }

   91  }

   72  func builtinSlashItems() []compItem {

   78  		})
   79  	}

   86  		if spec.showInHelp {
   87  			items = append(items, compItem{label: spec.name, hint: spec.hint})
   88  		}
   89  	}
   90  	return items
   91  }

   72  func builtinSlashItems() []compItem {
   73  	specs := builtinSlashSpecs()
   74  	items := make([]compItem, 0, len(specs))
   75  	for _, spec := range specs {
   76  		items = append(items, compItem{
   77  			label: spec.name, insert: spec.insert, hint: spec.hint, descend: spec.descend,

   83  func builtinSlashHelpItems() []compItem {
   84  	var items []compItem
   85  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   86  		if spec.showInHelp {
   87  			items = append(items, compItem{label: spec.name, hint: spec.hint})
   88  		}
   89  	}
   90  	return items
   91  }
   92
   93  func canonicalBuiltinSlashCommand(name string) string {
   94  	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
   95  	for _, spec := range builtinSlashSpecs() {
   96  		if name == spec.name {
   97  			return spec.name
   98  		}
   99  		if slices.Contains(spec.aliases, name) {
  100  			return spec.name
  101  		}
  102  	}
  103  	return name
  104  }

03 · COURSE ROUTE

十一章教程不是摘要，而是源码阅读路线

每章会从本项目的多个 finding 中挑出证据，先讲问题，再给比喻，然后把实现、边界、图谱和执行语义串起来。

M01

总览：先知道它解决什么问题

终端、桌面和服务端不是各写一套 Agent，而是都插到同一个“总电闸”上，所以权限、工具和生命周期不会因为换界面而变一套。

01
DIMENSION · ARCHITECTURE

架构与所有权

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · Boot 是唯一装配根，所有前端共享同一套 Harness

源码事实

boot 包明确负责加载配置、解析模型、构造内建工具与插件、接入权限 gate、构造 executor，并可包装双模型 Coordinator；TUI、HTTP/SSE、桌面 webview 都通过同一个 Controller 驱动。

白话解释

终端、桌面和服务端不是各写一套 Agent，而是都插到同一个“总电闸”上，所以权限、工具和生命周期不会因为换界面而变一套。

对自研 Harness 的含义

自研时应把 frontend 变成事件消费者，避免在 UI 层重复实现 turn、审批和恢复。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–3
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 4–8
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

    2  // it loads config, resolves the model(s), builds the tool registry (built-ins +

   63  		return agent.KeepErrors
   64  	}
   65  	var p agent.KeepPolicy
   66  	for _, k := range keep {
   67  		switch strings.TrimSpace(k) {

   69  			p |= agent.KeepErrors
   70  		case "user_marked":
   71  			p |= agent.KeepUserMarked
   72  		}
   73  	}
   74  	return p
   75  }

02
L1factarchitecture

02 · 主循环以模型自然结束为主，额外叠加多种止损护栏

源码事实

单个工具结果先被截到 32 KiB；空最终回复、流恢复、handoff 和 final readiness 各有上限。Agent 在 MaxSteps<=0 时不按轮数截断，直到模型给出无工具的 final、取消或 provider 错误；Delivery 模式还要求 todo、验证和 complete_step 证据。

白话解释

它允许长任务一直做，但每个工具输出和“最后确认”都有保险丝；只要交付模式缺验收或验证，主机就不会让它假装完成。

对自研 Harness 的含义

“无限循环”与“无限输出”被拆开治理；建设时要同时设计自然终止、重复调用 guard、输出预算和交付完成判定。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 33–46
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 47–62
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/agent/agent.go:33–62固定提交 ↗
   33  // maxToolOutputBytes caps a single tool result before it goes into the model's
   34  // context. ~32KB is roughly 8K tokens — enough for a full file read or a busy
   35  // grep, while preventing one accidental "read this 5 MB log" from blowing the
   36  // window before the next compaction runs.
   37  const maxToolOutputBytes = 32 * 1024
   38
   39  const maxFinalReadinessBlocks = 3
   40
   41  // maxFinalReadinessBlocksWithProgress is the hard cap on readiness retries when
   42  // the model keeps producing new host-observable receipts between blocks. A
   43  // converging turn (edit → verify → review still catching up to the latest
   44  // mutation) deserves more nudges than a stuck one; a turn that stalls with no
   45  // new receipts still fails at maxFinalReadinessBlocks.
   46  const maxFinalReadinessBlocksWithProgress = 6
   47  const maxEmptyFinalBlocks = 3
   48  const maxStreamRecoveries = 3
   49  const maxExecutorHandoffNudges = 1
   50
   51  // DeliveryRuntimeMarker is the delivery-mode contract block appended to user
   52  // turns (withTurnPreferences). Exported as the single source of truth for the
   53  // byte-exact suffix strip in preview derivation and for cross-package tests;
   54  // its text is cache-frozen — changing it breaks steer replay matching and the
   55  // prefix stability of every live delivery session.
   56  const DeliveryRuntimeMarker = `<delivery-runtime>
   57  This session is in delivery-first mode. Before any state-changing tool call,
   58  establish concrete, verifiable acceptance criteria with todo_write. After the
   59  change, inspect the result, run relevant verification, and sign off each step
   60  with complete_step citing the successful verification command. The host enforces
   61  these gates and will reject mutation or finalization when evidence is missing.
   62  </delivery-runtime>`
   63
   64  // Renderer redraws the assistant's final-answer text as styled output. It is
   65  // applied only after a turn's text stream completes, so the user sees raw
   66  // markdown stream live, then a single redraw replaces it with formatted
   67  // output. The renderer is intentionally interface-shaped so the agent stays
   68  // independent of the cli's markdown library choice. Consumed by TextSink.
   69  type Renderer interface {
   70  	Render(text string) string
   71  }
   72
   73  // Asker puts structured multiple-choice questions to the user and blocks for the
   74  // answers. The agent consults it for the `ask` tool. It is interface-shaped so
   75  // the agent stays independent of the frontend; a nil asker means no interactive
   76  // user (headless runs), where `ask` returns a "decide for yourself" result. The
   77  // interactive frontends wire the controller in as the Asker.
   78  type Asker interface {
   79  	Ask(ctx context.Context, questions []event.AskQuestion) ([]event.AskAnswer, error)
   80  }
   81
   82  // callContextKey carries the executing tool call's identity into Execute.
   83  type callContextKey struct{}
   84  type parentSessionContextKey struct{}
   85  type subagentDepthContextKey struct{}
   86  type userImagesContextKey struct{}
   87
   88  // callContext is the per-call context a tool can read. parentID is the call being
   89  // executed and sink is the agent's event sink (the `task` tool uses both to nest
   90  // a sub-agent's events under this call); asker lets the `ask` tool reach the user.
   91  type callContext struct {
   92  	parentID string
   93  	sink     event.Sink
   94  	asker    Asker
   95  	planMode bool
   96  }
   97
   98  // withCallContext stamps ctx with the executing call's ID, the agent's sink, and
   99  // the asker. executeOne sets this before every Execute; `task` reads it (via
  100  // CallContext) to nest sub-agent events, and `ask` reads the asker to prompt.
  101  // The plan-mode flag is mirrored onto the leaf planmode key so tools that must
  102  // not import this package (for example internal/tool/builtin) can still read it.
  103  func withCallContext(ctx context.Context, parentID string, sink event.Sink, asker Asker, planMode bool) context.Context {
  104  	ctx = planmode.WithActive(ctx, planMode)
  105  	return context.WithValue(ctx, callContextKey{}, callContext{parentID: parentID, sink: sink, asker: asker, planMode: planMode})
  106  }
  107
  108  // WithToolCallContext stamps ctx as a host-initiated top-level tool call.
  109  // Normal model-selected tools receive this context from executeOne; controller
  110  // entry points that deliberately invoke the same tool machinery (for example a
  111  // user typing /<subagent-skill>) use this exported wrapper so nested sub-agent
  112  // activity still reaches the parent event stream and plan-mode policy remains
  113  // visible to the invoked runner.
  114  func WithToolCallContext(ctx context.Context, parentID string, sink event.Sink, asker Asker, planMode bool) context.Context {
  115  	return withCallContext(ctx, parentID, sink, asker, planMode)
  116  }
  117
  118  // CallContext returns the executing call's ID, the agent's sink, and the asker,
  119  // if the context was set by an agent's executeOne. ok is false for a plain
  120  // context (headless tool tests, calls made outside the run loop).
  121  func CallContext(ctx context.Context) (parentID string, sink event.Sink, asker Asker, ok bool) {
  122  	cc, ok := ctx.Value(callContextKey{}).(callContext)
  123  	if !ok {
  124  		return "", nil, nil, false
  125  	}
  126  	return cc.parentID, cc.sink, cc.asker, true
  127  }
  128
  129  // PlanModeFromContext reports whether the tool call is executing during the
  130  // plan-first workflow. Tools may use it for phase-specific behavior, but it is
  131  // not a permission or read-only boundary.
  132  func PlanModeFromContext(ctx context.Context) bool {
  133  	cc, ok := ctx.Value(callContextKey{}).(callContext)

   59  // DeliveryRuntimeMarker is the delivery-mode contract block appended to user
   60  // turns (withTurnPreferences). Exported as the single source of truth for the
   61  // byte-exact suffix strip in preview derivation and for cross-package tests;
   62  // its text is cache-frozen — changing it breaks steer replay matching and the
   63  // prefix stability of every live delivery session.
   64  const DeliveryRuntimeMarker = `<delivery-runtime>
   65  This session is in delivery-first mode. Before any state-changing tool call,
   66  establish concrete, verifiable acceptance criteria with todo_write. After the
   67  change, inspect the result, run relevant verification, and sign off each step
   68  with complete_step citing the successful verification command. The host enforces
   69  these gates and will reject mutation or finalization when evidence is missing.
   70  </delivery-runtime>`
   71
   72  // Renderer redraws the assistant's final-answer text as styled output. It is
   73  // applied only after a turn's text stream completes, so the user sees raw
   74  // markdown stream live, then a single redraw replaces it with formatted
   75  // output. The renderer is intentionally interface-shaped so the agent stays
   76  // independent of the cli's markdown library choice. Consumed by TextSink.
   77  type Renderer interface {
   78  	Render(text string) string
   79  }
   80

   84  // user (headless runs), where `ask` returns a "decide for yourself" result. The
   85  // interactive frontends wire the controller in as the Asker.

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · Controller 对并发 turn、旋转、收尾和自动保存有明确状态机

源码事实

Controller 拒绝同一会话并发前台 turn；运行中可选择 park FIFO，finishing 窗口仍保持 admission 关闭，TurnDone 派发完成后才启动排队 turn；rotating 与 running 互斥，避免会话切换与执行产生 TOCTOU。

白话解释

用户连按几次发送不会把同一个会话撕成两半：新消息要么排队，要么明确被拒；切换会话时也不会恰好换掉正在用的那份上下文。

对自研 Harness 的含义

桌面/HTTP 多入口必须把“正在运行、等待审批、后台任务、收尾”分开建模，不能只暴露一个 running 布尔值。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 60–67
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 68–76
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  139  	responseLanguage       string
  140  	reasoningLanguage      string
  141  	disableColdResumePrune bool // legacy; rewrite elision removed, still gates cold notice
  142  	// testCacheColdAfter overrides cacheColdAfter() in tests. Zero uses the
  143  	// vendor-aware resolution from config.
  144  	testCacheColdAfter time.Duration
  145
  146  	shell                             sandbox.Shell                    // interpreter for user-invoked "!" commands; zero = auto
  147  	startedOnce                       bool                             // guards the one-shot SessionStart hook on first turn
03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

模型与上下文

7 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factmodelContext

01 · Provider 消息对象把模型内容与本地显示元数据分开

源码事实

provider.Message 同时承载正文、Raw/ProviderContent、图片、reasoning_content、Anthropic 签名、tool calls 和 LocalOnly；ModelMessages 会在发请求前移除 LocalOnly、RawContent 和 provider-only metadata，并在健康历史上原样返回底层切片以保留缓存路径。

白话解释

用户看到的半截流式输出可以保存下来，但它不会偷偷再喂给模型；真正发到 API 的内容和本地 UI 账本是两条线。

对自研 Harness 的含义

本地可观测性、恢复和 prompt cache 可以同时成立；模型可见消息类型必须有单独的 wire sanitizer。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 40–56
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 57–75
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  111  // ParseImageDataURL splits a `data:<media-type>;base64,<payload>` URL into its
  112  // media type and base64 payload. ok is false for anything that isn't a base64
  113  // data URL — providers that need the split (Anthropic) skip those silently.
  114  func ParseImageDataURL(dataURL string) (mediaType, base64Data string, ok bool) {
  115  	rest, found := strings.CutPrefix(dataURL, "data:")
  116  	if !found {
  117  		return "", "", false
  118  	}
  119  	meta, payload, found := strings.Cut(rest, ",")
  120  	if !found {
  121  		return "", "", false
  122  	}
  123  	mt, found := strings.CutSuffix(meta, ";base64")
  124  	if !found || mt == "" {
  125  		return "", "", false
  126  	}
  127  	return mt, payload, true
  128  }

  142  	// calls such as use_capability. Provider request builders deliberately
  143  	// serialize only provider-visible fields, so these values never alter the
  144  	// provider-visible conversation or prompt-cache prefix.
  145  	ResolvedName     string `json:"resolved_name,omitempty"`
  146  	CapabilityID     string `json:"capability_id,omitempty"`

02
L1factmodelContext

02 · 发请求前会修复 tool-call 配对和被截断的 JSON

源码事实

NormalizeMessages 在 wire 层补齐未回答的 assistant tool call、丢弃孤儿 tool result、恢复缺失工具名，并对半流式截断参数闭合 JSON；健康历史走 zero-allocation fast path，存盘历史使用保守的 NormalizeSessionMessages。

白话解释

模型上次说“我要调用工具”但进程崩了，下一次请求前会补一张“没有结果”的占位回执，避免 API 因为票据对不上直接 400。

对自研 Harness 的含义

恢复协议应在 provider 边界做防御性修复，而不是污染用户保存的原始 session。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 179–184
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 185–192
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/provider/provider.go:179–192固定提交 ↗
  179  // interruptedToolResult stands in for a tool result that never landed — an
  180  // assistant tool_calls turn whose execution was cut short (interrupt, crash) and
  181  // later resumed. Sending such a turn unanswered trips the OpenAI/DeepSeek 400
  182  // "An assistant message with 'tool_calls' must be followed by tool messages
  183  // responding to each 'tool_call_id'".
  184  const interruptedToolResult = "[no result: the previous turn was interrupted before this tool call completed]"
  185
  186  // SanitizeToolPairing is the provider-side alias for NormalizeMessages. It repairs
  187  // a history so it satisfies the tool-call contract the OpenAI-compatible and
  188  // Anthropic APIs enforce (every assistant tool_calls answered, no orphan tool
  189  // messages, truncated args closed) right before sending it to the wire — without
  190  // touching the stored session. Kept as a distinct name so call sites read as
  191  // "defensive wire prep" rather than "session mutation".
  192  func SanitizeToolPairing(msgs []Message) []Message { return NormalizeMessages(msgs) }
  193
  194  // ModelMessages removes durable display-only records before a request is
  195  // handed to any provider. Healthy sessions without such records keep their
  196  // original backing slice, preserving the allocation and prompt-cache fast path.
  197  func ModelMessages(msgs []Message) []Message {
  198  	needsCopy := false
  199  	for _, m := range msgs {
  200  		if m.LocalOnly || m.RawContent != "" || m.ProviderContent != "" {
  201  			needsCopy = true
  202  			break
  203  		}
  204  	}
  205  	if !needsCopy {
  206  		return msgs
  207  	}
  208  	out := make([]Message, 0, len(msgs))
  209  	for _, candidate := range msgs {
  210  		if candidate.LocalOnly {
  211  			continue
  212  		}
  213  		if candidate.ProviderContent != "" {
  214  			candidate.Content = candidate.ProviderContent
  215  			candidate.ProviderContent = ""
  216  		}
  217  		candidate.RawContent = ""
  218  		out = append(out, candidate)
  219  	}
  220  	return out
  221  }
  222
  223  // NormalizeMessages repairs a conversation history so it satisfies the tool-call
  224  // contract the OpenAI-compatible and Anthropic APIs enforce: every assistant
  225  // tool_calls entry must be answered by a following tool message for its id, and a
  226  // tool message must follow such a call. It backfills a placeholder result for any
  227  // unanswered call (so the turn stays intact), drops orphan tool messages,
  228  // backfills empty tool-call names from their results (#4727 — old sessions saved
  229  // before adde2d3e can carry an empty name), and closes truncated call-argument
  230  // JSON (DeepSeek 400s on replayed half-streamed args, #3953).
  231  //
  232  // This is the wire-safe entry point for provider requests. Stored session loads
  233  // use NormalizeSessionMessages so they can share the assistant-turn repairs
  234  // without deleting standalone tool messages that must round-trip through
  235  // reasonix --resume.
  236  //
  237  // A well-formed history — no unanswered calls, no orphan results, no empty tool-
  238  // call names, no truncated args — returns the input slice unchanged (same backing
  239  // array, zero allocation). This keeps the prefix-cache key stable for healthy
  240  // sessions and makes repeated normalization cheap.
  241  func NormalizeMessages(msgs []Message) []Message {
  242  	return normalizeMessages(msgs, true)
  243  }
  244
  245  // NormalizeSessionMessages applies only repairs that are safe to persist in a
  246  // saved session. It shares assistant-turn repairs with NormalizeMessages, but
  247  // preserves existing tool messages instead of dropping or reordering them so
  248  // Save/LoadSession remains a byte-for-byte conversation round trip for histories
  249  // that were already on disk.
  250  func NormalizeSessionMessages(msgs []Message) []Message {
  251  	return normalizeMessages(msgs, false)
  252  }
  253
  254  func normalizeMessages(msgs []Message, dropOrphanTools bool) []Message {
  255  	if normalized, ok := tryNormalizeFastPath(msgs, dropOrphanTools); ok {
  256  		return normalized // well-formed: pass through without allocating
  257  	}
  258  	out := make([]Message, 0, len(msgs))
  259  	for i := 0; i < len(msgs); {
  260  		m := msgs[i]
  261  		if m.LocalOnly {
  262  			if !dropOrphanTools {
  263  				out = append(out, m)

  193  	ThoughtSignature string `json:"thought_signature,omitempty"`
  194  	Diff             string `json:"diff,omitempty"`

  198  	// calls such as use_capability. Provider request builders deliberately
  199  	// serialize only provider-visible fields, so these values never alter the
  200  	// provider-visible conversation or prompt-cache prefix.

  224  }

  231  }

  233  // Auto ladder for max_output_tokens=0. Bounds completion only; never compact_ratio.
  234  const (
  235  	DefaultOrdinaryOutputTokens      = 16 * 1024  // non-reasoning
  236  	DefaultReasoningOutputTokens     = 32 * 1024  // ordinary reasoning
  237  	DefaultHighReasoningOutputTokens = 64 * 1024  // high/max effort
  238  	DefaultHighOutputTokens          = 128 * 1024 // explicit only; never auto
  239  )
  240
  241  // AutoOutputBudget maps max_output_tokens=0 to 16K/32K/64K by reasoning effort.
  242  func AutoOutputBudget(reasoningEnabled bool, effort string) int {
  243  	if !reasoningEnabled {
  244  		return DefaultOrdinaryOutputTokens
  245  	}
  246  	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(effort)) {
  247  	case "high", "max":
  248  		return DefaultHighReasoningOutputTokens
  249  	default:
  250  		return DefaultReasoningOutputTokens
  251  	}
  252  }

  255  // specific temperature, including 0 for deterministic output, can distinguish

03
L1factmodelContext

03 · DeepSeek thinking 与工具调用 reasoning replay 是显式协议分支

源码事实

OpenAI-compatible client 对 DeepSeek 标记 RequiresToolCallReasoning，并按模型/配置决定是否警告缺失 reasoning；构造请求时对包含 tool calls 的 assistant 消息发送 reasoning_content（包括空字符串），同时支持 DeepSeek Beta prefix continuation 和最多三次尚未输出 token 的断流重连。

白话解释

Reasonix 没把 DeepSeek 当普通 OpenAI 接口：它记得“思考字段”要跟着工具调用回放，并把 Beta 截断续写和断网重试接在同一个流上。

对自研 Harness 的含义

如果自研只保存 text/tool_calls 而丢 reasoning，DeepSeek thinking 模型会在下一轮请求失败或失去缓存命中。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 279–296
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 297–316
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  345  func cleanCustomHeaders(in map[string]string) map[string]string {
  346  	if len(in) == 0 {
  347  		return nil
  348  	}
  349  	out := make(map[string]string, len(in))
  350  	for rawName, rawValue := range in {
  351  		name := strings.TrimSpace(rawName)
  352  		value := strings.TrimSpace(rawValue)
  353  		if name == "" || value == "" || reservedCustomHeader(name) {
  354  			continue
  355  		}
  356  		out[name] = value
  357  	}
  358  	if len(out) == 0 {
  359  		return nil
  360  	}
  361  	return out
  362  }
  363
  364  func applyCustomHeaders(h http.Header, headers map[string]string) {
  365  	for name, value := range cleanCustomHeaders(headers) {
  366  		h.Set(name, value)
  367  	}
  368  }

  374  	}

  377  		return
  378  	}
  379  	h.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+apiKey)
  380  }

04
L1factmodelContext

04 · Anthropic 与 DeepSeek Anthropic 端点共用抽象但保留签名差异

源码事实

Anthropic provider 使用手写 net/http SSE；原生 Anthropic 的 thinking block 需要签名回放，而 DeepSeek Anthropic 端点使用 unsigned thinking、thinking.type 与 output_config.effort，client 通过 deepseek 标志分别处理。

白话解释

同一套 Message 能接两种协议，但不会把 Anthropic 的签名规则硬套到 DeepSeek 兼容端点上。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Provider adapter 应该把“协议共性”和“供应商必须回放的证明字段”拆开，不要用一套简单 JSON 模板覆盖所有模型。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–9
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 10–20
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/provider/anthropic/anthropic.go:1–20固定提交 ↗
    1  // Package anthropic implements the Anthropic Messages API provider (POST
    2  // /v1/messages, SSE streaming) with a hand-written net/http client — no SDK. It
    3  // self-registers under the "anthropic" kind, so any Claude model is a config
    4  // instance rather than code.
    5  //
    6  // Two notes, both rooted in the transport-agnostic provider.Message abstraction:
    7  //
    8  //   - Extended thinking is opt-in (provider config thinking="adaptive"). Anthropic
    9  //     requires the *signed* thinking block be replayed on the next turn when a tool
   10  //     call followed thinking, so Message carries ReasoningSignature alongside
   11  //     ReasoningContent and this provider replays the signed block on the next
   12  //     request. DeepSeek's Anthropic endpoint instead uses unsigned thinking blocks,
   13  //     thinking.type enabled|disabled, and output_config.effort; requests carrying
   14  //     tools must replay all provider reasoning. Some other compatible gateways such
   15  //     as LongCat use the binary toggle without output_config. (redacted_thinking
   16  //     blocks are not yet captured/replayed.)
   17  //   - Native Anthropic requests omit temperature/top_p. Current Claude models
   18  //     (Opus 4.8/4.7) reject sampling parameters with a 400; Anthropic steers
   19  //     behavior via prompting instead. DeepSeek's compatible endpoint accepts the
   20  //     caller's temperature, so that field is preserved only for DeepSeek.
   21  package anthropic
   22
   23  import (
   24  	"bufio"
   25  	"bytes"
   26  	"context"
   27  	"encoding/json"
   28  	"fmt"
   29  	"net/http"
   30  	"strings"
   31  	"sync"
   32  	"sync/atomic"
   33  	"time"
   34
   35  	"reasonix/internal/netclient"
   36  	"reasonix/internal/provider"
   37  	"reasonix/internal/provider/openai"
   38  )
   39
   40  // defaultStreamIdleTimeout caps how long a started SSE stream may go silent before
   41  // it's treated as a dropped connection — a half-open TCP connection (proxy switched
   42  // mid-stream) sends no RST, so scanner.Scan() would block forever. Generous on
   43  // purpose; live streams emit far more often. Stored per-client (client.idleTimeout)
   44  // so a test can shorten it without a shared global that races other watchdogs.
   45  const defaultStreamIdleTimeout = 120 * time.Second
   46
   47  const (
   48  	// anthropicVersion is the required API version header value.
   49  	anthropicVersion = "2023-06-01"
   50  	// defaultBaseURL is the first-party endpoint; config may override it (e.g. a
   51  	// gateway). Bedrock/Vertex use a different request shape and are out of scope.
   52  	defaultBaseURL = "https://api.anthropic.com"
   53  	// defaultMaxTokens is the output ceiling used when the request leaves MaxTokens
   54  	// unset. Anthropic *requires* max_tokens, and the agent currently doesn't set
   55  	// it, so this is the de-facto cap. Generous (you only pay for tokens actually
   56  	// produced) and within every catalog model's limit (Sonnet/Haiku 64K, Opus 128K).
   57  	defaultMaxTokens = 32768
   58  )
   59
   60  func init() {
   61  	provider.Register("anthropic", New)
   62  }
   63
   64  // New builds an Anthropic provider from a resolved config.
   65  func New(cfg provider.Config) (provider.Provider, error) {
   66  	if cfg.Model == "" {
   67  		return nil, fmt.Errorf("anthropic: model is required for provider %q", cfg.Name)
   68  	}
   69  	name := cfg.Name
   70  	if name == "" {
   71  		name = "anthropic"
   72  	}
   73  	baseURL := cfg.BaseURL
   74  	if baseURL == "" {
   75  		baseURL = defaultBaseURL
   76  	}
   77  	keyEnv, _ := cfg.Extra["api_key_env"].(string) // for actionable auth errors
   78  	keySource, _ := cfg.Extra["api_key_source"].(string)
   79  	thinking, _ := cfg.Extra["thinking"].(string)
   80  	thinking = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(thinking))
   81  	effort, _ := cfg.Extra["effort"].(string)
   82  	effort = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(effort))
   83  	vision, _ := cfg.Extra["vision"].(bool)
   84  	headers, _ := cfg.Extra["headers"].(map[string]string)
   85  	authHeader, _ := cfg.Extra["auth_header"].(bool)
   86  	httpClient, err := newHTTPClient(cfg)
   87  	if err != nil {
   88  		return nil, fmt.Errorf("anthropic: network: %w", err)
   89  	}
   90  	// Anthropic's API surface is at {root}/v1/messages, so c.baseURL stores
   91  	// the *root* — without any trailing /v1. The setup wizard, however, lets

internal/provider/anthropic/anthropic.go · 9aaf8d381a21当前源码 ↗
    1  // Package anthropic implements the Anthropic Messages API provider (POST
    2  // /v1/messages, SSE streaming) with a hand-written net/http client — no SDK. It
    3  // self-registers under the "anthropic" kind, so any Claude model is a config
    4  // instance rather than code.
    5  //
    6  // Two notes, both rooted in the transport-agnostic provider.Message abstraction:
    7  //
    8  //   - Extended thinking is opt-in (provider config thinking="adaptive"). Anthropic
    9  //     requires the *signed* thinking block be replayed on the next turn when a tool
   10  //     call followed thinking, so Message carries ReasoningSignature alongside
   11  //     ReasoningContent and this provider replays the signed block on the next
   12  //     request. DeepSeek's Anthropic endpoint instead uses unsigned thinking blocks,
   13  //     thinking.type enabled|disabled, and output_config.effort; requests carrying
   14  //     tools must replay all provider reasoning. Some other compatible gateways such
   15  //     as LongCat use the binary toggle without output_config. (redacted_thinking
   16  //     blocks are not yet captured/replayed.)
   17  //   - Native Anthropic requests omit temperature/top_p. Current Claude models
   18  //     (Opus 4.8/4.7) reject sampling parameters with a 400; Anthropic steers
   19  //     behavior via prompting instead. DeepSeek's compatible endpoint accepts the
   20  //     caller's temperature, so that field is preserved only for DeepSeek.
   21  package anthropic
   22
   23  import (
   24  	"bufio"
   25  	"bytes"
   26  	"context"
   27  	"encoding/json"
   28  	"fmt"
   29  	"io"
   30  	"net/http"
   31  	"strings"
   32  	"sync"
   33  	"sync/atomic"
   34  	"time"
   35
   36  	"reasonix/internal/netclient"
   37  	"reasonix/internal/provider"
   38  	"reasonix/internal/provider/openai"
   39  )
   40
   41  // defaultStreamIdleTimeout caps how long a started SSE stream may go silent before
   42  // it's treated as a dropped connection — a half-open TCP connection (proxy switched
   43  // mid-stream) sends no RST, so scanner.Scan() would block forever. Generous on
   44  // purpose; live streams emit far more often. Stored per-client (client.idleTimeout)
   45  // so a test can shorten it without a shared global that races other watchdogs.
   46  const defaultStreamIdleTimeout = 120 * time.Second
   47
   48  const (
   49  	// anthropicVersion is the required API version header value.
   50  	anthropicVersion = "2023-06-01"
   51  	// defaultBaseURL is the first-party endpoint; config may override it (e.g. a
   52  	// gateway). Bedrock/Vertex use a different request shape and are out of scope.
   53  	defaultBaseURL = "https://api.anthropic.com"
   54  	// defaultMaxTokens is the mandatory Anthropic fallback when neither config
   55  	// nor request supplies max_tokens. Ordinary turns use 16K; reasoning-capable
   56  	// paths raise via AutoOutputBudget. 128K is never automatic.
   57  	defaultMaxTokens = provider.DefaultOrdinaryOutputTokens
   58  )
   59
   60  func init() {
   61  	provider.Register("anthropic", New)
   62  }
   63
   64  // New builds an Anthropic provider from a resolved config.
   65  func New(cfg provider.Config) (provider.Provider, error) {
   66  	if cfg.Model == "" {
   67  		return nil, fmt.Errorf("anthropic: model is required for provider %q", cfg.Name)
   68  	}
   69  	name := cfg.Name
   70  	if name == "" {
   71  		name = "anthropic"
   72  	}
   73  	baseURL := cfg.BaseURL
   74  	if baseURL == "" {
   75  		baseURL = defaultBaseURL
   76  	}
   77  	// Anthropic's API surface is at {root}/v1/messages, so c.baseURL stores
   78  	// the *root* -- without any trailing /v1. The setup wizard, however, lets
   79  	// users paste a full OpenAI-compatible URL (e.g.
   80  	// "https://proxy.example.com/v1") because that's what /models probes
   81  	// expect. Stripping the trailing /v1 here makes both forms land on the
   82  	// same endpoint without forcing users to remember Anthropic's quirky
   83  	// root-vs-versioned split. Without this, a user pasting
   84  	// "https://proxy.example.com/v1" would probe /v1/models successfully
   85  	// but get the chat client concatenating onto
   86  	// "https://proxy.example.com/v1/v1/messages" -- a 404.
   87  	root := strings.TrimRight(baseURL, "/")
   88  	root = strings.TrimSuffix(root, "/v1")
   89  	if root == "" {
   90  		root = defaultBaseURL
   91  	}
05
L1factmodelContext

05 · 上下文维护是 0.5/0.6/0.8/0.9 多级管道

源码事实

默认 softCompactRatio=0.5、toolResultSnipRatio=0.6、compactRatio=0.8、forceRatio=0.9，tail 固定 16384 tokens；先提示并保持 cache prefix，再瘦身旧工具结果，必要时 prune 后摘要，连续两次仍压不下去则设置 compactStuck 暂停自动压缩。

白话解释

它不会一到 50% 就重写整段历史：先提醒、再剪掉过期工具输出，真的快满才摘要；窗口太小导致反复压缩时会熔断而不是死循环。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Context pipeline 应把成本、缓存稳定性、信息损失和熔断作为不同阈值测试，而不是单一 max-token 截断。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 19–26
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 27–36
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

internal/agent/compact.go:19–36固定提交 ↗
   19  // Compaction is a low-frequency cache-reset point: the prompt grows append-only
   20  // (high cache hits) until a turn nears compactRatio of the window, then it is
   21  // compacted down to a tail budget. The budget is a fixed token count, not a
   22  // fraction of the window, so a huge window still compacts rarely while a small
   23  // one still lands below the trigger (which is what stops the re-compaction loop).
   24  const (
   25  	defaultSoftCompactRatio    = 0.5   // report growing context here, but keep the cache-stable prefix intact
   26  	defaultToolResultSnipRatio = 0.6   // rewrite stale tool results cheaply before summary compaction
   27  	defaultCompactRatio        = 0.8   // trigger: prompt at this fraction of the window compacts
   28  	defaultCompactForceRatio   = 0.9   // force compaction at this high-water mark even for low-value folds
   29  	defaultCompactTarget       = 0.5   // safety cap: the kept tail never exceeds this fraction of the window
   30  	defaultTailTokens          = 16384 // verbatim recent-tail budget, in tokens
   31  	minRecentKeep              = 2     // never keep fewer recent messages than this
   32  	minCompactMessages         = 2     // skip compaction below this many compactable messages
   33  	fallbackTokPerChar         = 0.25  // ~4 chars/token, used before any usage is available to calibrate
   34  	maxPinnedFirstUserTokens   = 1500  // ceiling on pinning the first user turn verbatim; larger first turns (pasted content) stay foldable
   35  	pinnedFirstUserWindowFrac  = 0.15  // and never pin a first turn worth more than this fraction of the window
   36  )
   37
   38  // summaryTag wraps the compaction summary so the model can distinguish it from
   39  // live user input and later strip or skip it when reasoning about the current turn.
   40  const (
   41  	summaryTagOpen  = "<compaction-summary>"
   42  	summaryTagClose = "</compaction-summary>"
   43  )
   44
   45  // summaryTimeout bounds one summarizer call so a stalled stream surfaces a clear
   46  // failure (then a mechanical fold) instead of hanging compaction indefinitely.
   47  const summaryTimeout = 90 * time.Second
   48
   49  // summarySystemPrompt steers the executor to distill older history into a
   50  // structured briefing it can keep relying on after the originals are dropped.
   51  // The section layout mirrors what a coding agent actually needs to resume work
   52  // mid-task: the goal verbatim, the concrete state of the code, and an explicit
   53  // next step — so the post-compaction turn doesn't lose the thread or re-derive
   54  // decisions already made.
   55  const summarySystemPrompt = `You are compacting the earlier part of a coding agent's conversation to save context.
   56  The agent keeps your summary alongside the user's own turns (kept verbatim) and the recent tail; your job is to fold the assistant/tool work into a briefing it can resume from.
   57  Write under these exact headings, omitting a heading only if it has no content:
   58
   59  ## Standing facts & constraints
   60  Everything the user stated that still governs the work — names, paths, IDs, versions, tokens, preferences, and hard "never do X" rules — in their own words. Be exhaustive; this is the durable contract, so prefer over- to under-including.
   61
   62  ## Goal
   63  The user's request and intent.
   64
   65  ## Decisions & rationale
   66  Key choices made so far and why — so they are not re-litigated or reversed.
   67
   68  ## Files & code
   69  Files read or modified, with the specific facts that matter: signatures, line locations, data shapes, and exact edits applied. Be concrete; this is what lets the agent act without re-reading everything.
   70
   71  ## Commands & outcomes
   72  Commands run (builds, tests, git) and their relevant results — what passed, what failed, and the error text that matters.
   73
   74  ## Errors & fixes
   75  Problems hit and how they were resolved (or not), so the same dead ends are not repeated.
   76
   77  ## Pending & next step
   78  What is still in progress or unstarted, and the single most concrete next action to take.
   79
   80  Rules: be terse — bullet points and fragments, not prose. Preserve identifiers, paths, and numbers exactly. Do NOT invent anything not present in the messages; if something is unknown, leave it out rather than guessing.`
   81
   82  // maybeCompact compacts the session when the last turn's prompt has grown to the
   83  // configured fraction of the context window. It is a no-op when compaction is
   84  // disabled (no window) or usage is unavailable.
   85  func (a *Agent) maybeCompact(ctx context.Context, u *provider.Usage) {
   86  	if a.contextWindow <= 0 || u == nil || u.PromptTokens == 0 {
   87  		return
   88  	}
   89  	high := int(float64(a.contextWindow) * a.compactRatio)
   90  	snip := int(float64(a.contextWindow) * a.toolResultSnipRatio)
   91  	soft := int(float64(a.contextWindow) * a.softCompactRatio)
   92  	// Between the soft ratio and the trigger, report growing context once without
   93  	// rewriting the prefix — a compaction here would needlessly crater the cache.
   94  	if u.PromptTokens >= soft && u.PromptTokens < snip && !a.softCompactNoticed {
   95  		a.softCompactNoticed = true
   96  		detail := fmt.Sprintf("context reached %.0f%% of window; keeping cache-first prefix until compact threshold %.0f%%", a.softCompactRatio*100, a.compactRatio*100)
   97  		a.sink.Emit(event.Event{Kind: event.Notice, Level: event.LevelInfo, Text: "Context is getting large; preserving cache until cleanup is needed.", Detail: detail})
   98  		return
   99  	}
  100  	if u.PromptTokens >= snip && u.PromptTokens < high {
  101  		ratio := a.tokPerChar()
  102  		if st, err := a.SnipStaleToolResults(); err == nil && st.Results > 0 {
  103  			saved := int(float64(st.SavedChars) * ratio)
  104  			a.sink.Emit(event.Event{Kind: event.Notice, Level: event.LevelInfo, Text: fmt.Sprintf(
  105  				"snipped %d stale tool results (~%d tokens est.) before compaction", st.Results, saved)})
  106  		}
  107  		return

internal/agent/compact.go · 9aaf8d381a21当前源码 ↗
   19  // Compaction is a low-frequency cache-reset point: the prompt grows append-only
   20  // until compactRatio of the window is crossed, then one content-driven summary
   21  // checkpoint is installed (stable prefix + one structured digest + recent tail).
   22  // 50% is only the normal acceptance ceiling — candidates are never padded up to it.
   23  const (
   24  	defaultCompactRatio        = 0.85 // sole automatic maintenance trigger (new configs)
   25  	checkpointCeilingRatio     = 0.50 // normal auto-checkpoint acceptance ceiling
   26  	recentTailBudgetRatio      = 0.10 // recent verbatim tail as a fraction of the window
   27  	minRecentTailTokens        = 32 * 1024
   28  	maxRecentTailTokens        = 96 * 1024
   29  	summaryOutputMaxTokens     = 16 * 1024 // max digest output; further clipped by remaining candidate space
   30  	exceptionalMinSavingsRatio = 0.25      // when fixed prefix alone exceeds 50%, require at least this savings
   31  	minRecentKeep              = 2         // never keep fewer recent messages than this
   32  	minCompactMessages         = 2         // skip compaction below this many compactable messages
   33  	fallbackTokPerChar         = 0.25      // ~4 chars/token, used before any usage is available to calibrate
   34  	maxPinnedFirstUserTokens   = 1500      // ceiling on pinning the first user turn verbatim
   35  	pinnedFirstUserWindowFrac  = 0.15      // and never pin a first turn worth more than this fraction of the window
   36  	maxKeptUserTurnTokens      = 1500      // ceiling on carrying one folded user turn verbatim
   37  	keptUserTurnsBudgetTokens  = 8192      // and on all of them together within one fold
   38  	keptUserTurnsWindowFrac    = 0.05      // never spend more than this fraction of the window on them
   39  	protocolReserveTokens      = 256       // provider framing and control fields not represented by message estimates
   40  )
   41
   42  var (
   43  	errSummaryOutputTruncated = errors.New("summarizer output truncated")
   44  	errCheckpointRejected     = errors.New("checkpoint candidate rejected")
   45  )
   46
   47  // summaryTag wraps the compaction summary so the model can distinguish it from
   48  // live user input and later strip or skip it when reasoning about the current turn.

   50  	summaryTagOpen  = "<compaction-summary>"
   51  	summaryTagClose = "</compaction-summary>"

   63

   79  ## Errors & fixes
   80  Problems hit and how they were resolved (or not), so the same dead ends are not repeated.
   81
   82  ## Pending & next step
   83  What is still in progress or unstarted, and the single most concrete next action to take.
   84
   85  Rules: be terse — bullet points and fragments, not prose. Preserve identifiers, paths, and numbers exactly. Do NOT invent anything not present in the messages; if something is unknown, leave it out rather than guessing.`
   86
   87  // compactTrigger is the sole automatic context-maintenance boundary. Output
   88  // budgets are intentionally absent: they are clipped against the final request
   89  // at send time and must never make compaction happen earlier than the user's
   90  // configured compact_ratio.
   91  func (a *Agent) compactTrigger() int {
   92  	if a == nil || a.contextWindow <= 0 {
   93  		return 0
   94  	}
   95  	ratio := a.compactRatio
   96  	if ratio <= 0 {
   97  		ratio = defaultCompactRatio
   98  	}
   99  	if a.ablation.Off(ablation.Compaction) {
  100  		ratio = 0.5
  101  	}
  102  	return max(1, int(float64(a.contextWindow)*ratio))
  103  }
  104
  105  // hardInputCeiling is a physical input-safety boundary, not another user
  106  // compaction threshold. Reply budgets are resolved independently at send time.
  107  func (a *Agent) hardInputCeiling() int {
06
L1factmodelContext

06 · 摘要保留用户事实、最近尾部并归档完整旧历史

源码事实

summarySystemPrompt 要求保留 standing facts、goal、decisions、files/code、commands/outcomes、errors/fixes、pending/next step；compact 会把可折叠区域先 archive，再摘要，摘要失败时使用机械 fold marker，保留小 user turns 和完整 active turn。

白话解释

压缩不是把聊天变成一句“继续工作”：它把用户硬约束、做过的命令、错误和下一步分栏记录，原始旧消息还留在 archive 里。

对自研 Harness 的含义

摘要 schema 本身是 Harness 的恢复协议，应该有 golden tests，不能完全交给模型自由发挥。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 49–63
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 64–80
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/agent/compact.go:49–80固定提交 ↗
   49  // summarySystemPrompt steers the executor to distill older history into a
   50  // structured briefing it can keep relying on after the originals are dropped.
   51  // The section layout mirrors what a coding agent actually needs to resume work
   52  // mid-task: the goal verbatim, the concrete state of the code, and an explicit
   53  // next step — so the post-compaction turn doesn't lose the thread or re-derive
   54  // decisions already made.
   55  const summarySystemPrompt = `You are compacting the earlier part of a coding agent's conversation to save context.
   56  The agent keeps your summary alongside the user's own turns (kept verbatim) and the recent tail; your job is to fold the assistant/tool work into a briefing it can resume from.
   57  Write under these exact headings, omitting a heading only if it has no content:
   58
   59  ## Standing facts & constraints
   60  Everything the user stated that still governs the work — names, paths, IDs, versions, tokens, preferences, and hard "never do X" rules — in their own words. Be exhaustive; this is the durable contract, so prefer over- to under-including.
   61
   62  ## Goal
   63  The user's request and intent.
   64
   65  ## Decisions & rationale
   66  Key choices made so far and why — so they are not re-litigated or reversed.
   67
   68  ## Files & code
   69  Files read or modified, with the specific facts that matter: signatures, line locations, data shapes, and exact edits applied. Be concrete; this is what lets the agent act without re-reading everything.
   70
   71  ## Commands & outcomes
   72  Commands run (builds, tests, git) and their relevant results — what passed, what failed, and the error text that matters.
   73
   74  ## Errors & fixes
   75  Problems hit and how they were resolved (or not), so the same dead ends are not repeated.
   76
   77  ## Pending & next step
   78  What is still in progress or unstarted, and the single most concrete next action to take.
   79
   80  Rules: be terse — bullet points and fragments, not prose. Preserve identifiers, paths, and numbers exactly. Do NOT invent anything not present in the messages; if something is unknown, leave it out rather than guessing.`
   81
   82  // maybeCompact compacts the session when the last turn's prompt has grown to the
   83  // configured fraction of the context window. It is a no-op when compaction is
   84  // disabled (no window) or usage is unavailable.
   85  func (a *Agent) maybeCompact(ctx context.Context, u *provider.Usage) {
   86  	if a.contextWindow <= 0 || u == nil || u.PromptTokens == 0 {
   87  		return
   88  	}
   89  	high := int(float64(a.contextWindow) * a.compactRatio)
   90  	snip := int(float64(a.contextWindow) * a.toolResultSnipRatio)
   91  	soft := int(float64(a.contextWindow) * a.softCompactRatio)
   92  	// Between the soft ratio and the trigger, report growing context once without
   93  	// rewriting the prefix — a compaction here would needlessly crater the cache.
   94  	if u.PromptTokens >= soft && u.PromptTokens < snip && !a.softCompactNoticed {
   95  		a.softCompactNoticed = true
   96  		detail := fmt.Sprintf("context reached %.0f%% of window; keeping cache-first prefix until compact threshold %.0f%%", a.softCompactRatio*100, a.compactRatio*100)
   97  		a.sink.Emit(event.Event{Kind: event.Notice, Level: event.LevelInfo, Text: "Context is getting large; preserving cache until cleanup is needed.", Detail: detail})
   98  		return
   99  	}
  100  	if u.PromptTokens >= snip && u.PromptTokens < high {
  101  		ratio := a.tokPerChar()
  102  		if st, err := a.SnipStaleToolResults(); err == nil && st.Results > 0 {
  103  			saved := int(float64(st.SavedChars) * ratio)
  104  			a.sink.Emit(event.Event{Kind: event.Notice, Level: event.LevelInfo, Text: fmt.Sprintf(
  105  				"snipped %d stale tool results (~%d tokens est.) before compaction", st.Results, saved)})
  106  		}
  107  		return
  108  	}
  109  	if u.PromptTokens < high {
  110  		// A turn that sits under the trigger is the breathing room a healthy
  111  		// compaction buys; it clears the stuck latch and the run counter.
  112  		a.consecutiveCompacts = 0
  113  		a.compactStuck = false
  114  		return
  115  	}
  116  	if a.compactStuck {
  117  		return
  118  	}
  119  	force := u.PromptTokens >= int(float64(a.contextWindow)*a.compactForceRatio)
  120  	// Prune before folding: when eliding stale tool results alone clears the
  121  	// trigger, this turn's (paid) summarize call is skipped entirely.
  122  	ratio := a.tokPerChar()
  123  	if st, err := a.PruneStaleToolResults(); err == nil && st.Results > 0 {
  124  		saved := int(float64(st.SavedChars) * ratio)
  125  		a.sink.Emit(event.Event{Kind: event.Notice, Level: event.LevelInfo, Text: fmt.Sprintf(
  126  			"pruned %d stale tool results (~%d tokens est.) before compaction", st.Results, saved)})
  127  		if !force && u.PromptTokens-saved < high {
  128  			return
  129  		}
  130  	}
  131  	if err := a.compact(ctx, "auto", "", force); err != nil {
  132  		a.sink.Emit(event.Event{Kind: event.Notice, Level: event.LevelInfo, Text: "Context cleanup skipped for now.", Detail: fmt.Sprintf("compaction skipped: %v", err)})
  133  		return
  134  	}
  135  	// A healthy compaction drops the prompt under the trigger, so the next turn
  136  	// won't compact. Compacting on consecutive turns means the kept tail alone
  137  	// exceeds the trigger — the system prompt plus one verbatim turn is bigger than
  138  	// the window allows. Re-firing every turn is the loop users hit, so pause
  139  	// auto-compaction and say why, once.
  140  	a.consecutiveCompacts++
  141  	if a.consecutiveCompacts >= 2 {
  142  		a.compactStuck = true
  143  		a.sink.Emit(event.Event{Kind: event.Notice, Level: event.LevelInfo, Text: "Automatic context cleanup paused because the context window is too small.", Detail: fmt.Sprintf(
  144  			"context_window=%d is too small for compaction to help (the system prompt plus one turn already exceeds %.0f%% of it); raise context_window or shrink tool output. Auto-compaction paused until the prompt drops.",
  145  			a.contextWindow, a.compactRatio*100)})
  146  	}
  147  }
  148
  149  // foldEconomics estimates whether compacting the given region saves enough
  150  // tokens to justify the summarization API call. It returns false when the
  151  // region is too small for the savings to outweigh the extra round-trip cost

  114  // recentTailBudget is the content-construction budget for the recent verbatim
  115  // tail. Production windows use clamp(window×10%, 32K, 96K). Smaller synthetic
  116  // windows (tests / constrained providers) drop the 32K floor so the tail cannot
  117  // alone exceed the window.
  118  func (a *Agent) recentTailBudget() int {
  119  	if a == nil || a.contextWindow <= 0 {
  120  		return minRecentTailTokens
  121  	}
  122  	n := int(float64(a.contextWindow) * recentTailBudgetRatio)
  123  	if a.contextWindow >= minRecentTailTokens*2 {
  124  		if n < minRecentTailTokens {
  125  			n = minRecentTailTokens
  126  		}
  127  	}
  128  	if n > maxRecentTailTokens {
  129  		n = maxRecentTailTokens
  130  	}
  131  	if max := a.contextWindow / 2; max > 0 && n > max {
  132  		n = max
  133  	}
  134  	return max(1, n)
  135  }
  136
  137  // checkpointCeiling is the normal auto-checkpoint acceptance upper bound
  138  // (50% of the window). Candidates below this are accepted without padding.
  139  func (a *Agent) checkpointCeiling() int {
  140  	if a == nil || a.contextWindow <= 0 {
  141  		return 0
  142  	}
  143  	return max(1, int(float64(a.contextWindow)*checkpointCeilingRatio))
  144  }
  145
  146  // exceptionalMinimumSavings is required only when the fixed prefix alone already
  147  // exceeds the 50% ceiling; otherwise ordinary candidates simply stay under 50%.
  148  func (a *Agent) exceptionalMinimumSavings() int {
  149  	if a == nil || a.contextWindow <= 0 {
  150  		return 0
  151  	}

L1factmodelContext

07 · 项目指令与记忆在启动时组成稳定 system prefix，编辑延迟到下一 session

源码事实

memory.Load 一次发现层级文档和自动记忆索引，Compose 把 base prompt 放在最前并追加 memory；WriteDoc 只允许写已识别的记忆文件，并明确不会改变当前 cache-stable system prefix，而是由 controller 通过 turn-tail note 临时生效。

白话解释

REASONIX/AGENTS/全局记忆像开机时装进机器的说明书；本轮改说明书不会偷偷改掉正在复用的缓存前缀，下一次 session 才完全换新。

对自研 Harness 的含义

指令层级和 cache key 需要一起设计；动态信息应走短暂 turn injection，不要频繁重写系统前缀。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 12–31
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 32–53
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/memory/memory.go:12–53固定提交 ↗
   12  // Set is everything memory loaded for one session: the hierarchical docs and a
   13  // handle to the auto-memory store (whose index is captured at load time). It is
   14  // assembled once at boot and folded into the system prompt by Compose. CWD and
   15  // UserDir are retained so the controller can resolve quick-add targets without
   16  // re-deriving discovery context.
   17  type Set struct {
   18  	Docs                   []Source // REASONIX.md / AGENTS.md, ascending precedence
   19  	GlobalGuidance         []Memory // stable snapshot of global user/feedback bodies
   20  	Store                  Store    // auto-memory store (may be a zero/disabled Store)
   21  	Index                  string   // MEMORY.md contents at load time
   22  	CWD                    string   // project working dir used for discovery
   23  	UserDir                string   // user config root (may be "")
   24  	InstructionDiagnostics []instruction.Diagnostic
   25  }
   26
   27  // Options configures discovery. CWD defaults to "." and UserDir is the user
   28  // config root (config.MemoryUserDir()); a "" UserDir disables user-global docs
   29  // and the auto-memory store.
   30  type Options struct {
   31  	CWD     string
   32  	UserDir string
   33  }
   34
   35  // Load discovers all memory for a session: the hierarchical docs and the
   36  // auto-memory index. It is best-effort and never errors — missing files just
   37  // mean less memory — so boot can call it unconditionally.
   38  func Load(opts Options) *Set {
   39  	cwd := opts.CWD
   40  	if cwd == "" {
   41  		cwd = "."
   42  	}
   43  	store := StoreFor(opts.UserDir, cwd)
   44  	resolved := instruction.Resolve(instruction.ResolveOptions{TargetDir: cwd, UserDir: opts.UserDir})
   45  	return &Set{
   46  		Docs:                   resolved.Documents,
   47  		GlobalGuidance:         store.globalGuidanceForProject(),
   48  		Store:                  store,
   49  		Index:                  store.Index(),
   50  		CWD:                    cwd,
   51  		UserDir:                opts.UserDir,
   52  		InstructionDiagnostics: resolved.Diagnostics,
   53  	}
   54  }
   55
   56  // DocPath returns the doc-memory file a given scope writes to. To avoid splitting
   57  // a project's memory across conventions, it prefers a file that already exists
   58  // (REASONIX.md / AGENTS.md / CLAUDE.md, in that order); when none exists it
   59  // creates the universal default (AGENTS.md / AGENTS.local.md). ScopeUser →
   60  // <userDir>, ScopeLocal → <cwd> with the *.local.md names, anything else → <cwd>.
   61  // Returns "" for ScopeUser when no user dir is configured.
   62  func (s *Set) DocPath(scope Scope) string {
   63  	dir := s.CWD
   64  	names, def := docNames, defaultDocName
   65  	switch scope {
   66  	case ScopeUser:
   67  		if s.UserDir == "" {
   68  			return ""
   69  		}
   70  		dir = s.UserDir
   71  	case ScopeLocal:
   72  		names, def = localNames, defaultLocalName
   73  	}
   74  	for _, n := range names {
   75  		p := filepath.Join(dir, n)
   76  		if _, err := os.Stat(p); err == nil {
   77  			return p // append to the doc already in use
   78  		}
   79  	}
   80  	return filepath.Join(dir, def)
   81  }
   82
   83  // Empty reports whether the set carries nothing to inject, so Compose can leave
   84  // the base prompt byte-for-byte untouched (and the cache prefix maximal) when
   85  // there is no memory at all.
   86  func (s *Set) Empty() bool {
   87  	return s == nil || (len(s.Docs) == 0 && len(s.GlobalGuidance) == 0 && strings.TrimSpace(s.Index) == "")
   88  }
   89
   90  // docScopes are the scopes the panel can target for a quick-add or a new doc.
   91  // Ordered broad → specific for display.
   92  var docScopes = []Scope{ScopeUser, ScopeProject, ScopeLocal}
   93
   94  // allowedDocPaths is the closed set of files WriteDoc / AppendDoc may touch: the
   95  // canonical file for each writable scope, plus every doc already discovered this
   96  // session (so an ancestor or AGENTS.md the user is already editing stays
   97  // editable). Keyed by absolute path. This bounds frontend-driven writes to real
   98  // memory files rather than arbitrary paths.
   99  func (s *Set) allowedDocPaths() map[string]bool {
  100  	allow := map[string]bool{}
  101  	for _, sc := range docScopes {
  102  		if p := s.DocPath(sc); p != "" {
  103  			allow[absOf(p)] = true
  104  		}
  105  	}
  106  	for _, d := range s.Docs {
  107  		allow[absOf(d.Path)] = true
  108  	}
  109  	return allow
  110  }
  111
  112  // WriteDoc overwrites a doc-memory file with body, after checking path is a
  113  // recognized memory file (see allowedDocPaths). It is the save side of the
  114  // desktop panel's in-place editor. The write lands on disk immediately but does
  115  // NOT mutate the cache-stable system prefix — the edit folds into the prefix on
  116  // the next session; to make it apply this session, the controller separately
  117  // queues a turn-tail note. Returns the path written.
  118  func (s *Set) WriteDoc(path, body string) (string, error) {
  119  	if s == nil {
  120  		return "", fmt.Errorf("memory unavailable")
  121  	}
  122  	if strings.TrimSpace(path) == "" {
  123  		return "", fmt.Errorf("no path given")
  124  	}

internal/memory/memory.go · 9aaf8d381a21当前源码 ↗
   12  // Set is everything memory loaded for one session: the hierarchical docs and a
   13  // handle to the auto-memory store (whose index is captured at load time). It is
   14  // assembled once at boot and folded into the system prompt by Compose. CWD and
   15  // UserDir are retained so the controller can resolve quick-add targets without
   16  // re-deriving discovery context.
   17  type Set struct {
   18  	Docs                   []Source // REASONIX.md / AGENTS.md, ascending precedence
   19  	PinnedGuidance         []Memory // stable snapshot of pinned fact bodies (incl. legacy global user/feedback)
   20  	Store                  Store    // auto-memory store (may be a zero/disabled Store)
   21  	Index                  string   // MEMORY.md contents at load time
   22  	CWD                    string   // project working dir used for discovery
   23  	UserDir                string   // user config root (may be "")
   24  	InstructionDiagnostics []instruction.Diagnostic
   25
   26  	// recall is the snapshot's prebuilt retrieval index (nil when memory is
   27  	// hidden or empty); Set.AutoRecall serves each turn from it without disk.
   28  	recall *RecallIndex
   29  }
   30
   31  // Options configures discovery. CWD defaults to "." and UserDir is the user
   32  // config root (config.MemoryUserDir()); a "" UserDir disables user-global docs
   33  // and the auto-memory store.
   34  type Options struct {
   35  	CWD     string
   36  	UserDir string
   37  }
   38
   39  // Load discovers all memory for a session: the hierarchical docs and the
   40  // auto-memory index. It is best-effort and never errors — missing files just
   41  // mean less memory — so boot can call it unconditionally.
   42  func Load(opts Options) *Set {
   43  	cwd := opts.CWD
   44  	if cwd == "" {
   45  		cwd = "."
   46  	}
   47  	resolved := instruction.Resolve(instruction.ResolveOptions{TargetDir: cwd, UserDir: opts.UserDir})
   48  	// MemoryBench's counterfactual arm: hide the store, index, pinned
   49  	// guidance, and recall so paired runs measure memory's contribution.
   50  	// Instruction docs stay — standing instructions are not under test.
   51  	if os.Getenv("REASONIX_EXPERIMENT_NO_MEMORY") == "1" {
   52  		return &Set{Docs: resolved.Documents, CWD: cwd, UserDir: opts.UserDir,
   53  			InstructionDiagnostics: resolved.Diagnostics}
   54  	}
   55  	store := StoreFor(opts.UserDir, cwd)
   56  	return &Set{
   57  		Docs:                   resolved.Documents,
   58  		PinnedGuidance:         store.pinnedGuidanceForProject(),
   59  		Store:                  store,
   60  		Index:                  store.Index(),
   61  		CWD:                    cwd,
   62  		UserDir:                opts.UserDir,
   63  		InstructionDiagnostics: resolved.Diagnostics,
   64  		recall:                 BuildRecallIndex(store),
   65  	}
   66  }
   67
   68  // DocPath returns the doc-memory file a given scope writes to. To avoid splitting
   69  // a project's memory across conventions, it prefers a file that already exists
   70  // (REASONIX.md / AGENTS.md / CLAUDE.md, in that order); when none exists it
   71  // creates the universal default (AGENTS.md / AGENTS.local.md). ScopeUser →
   72  // <userDir>, ScopeLocal → <cwd> with the *.local.md names, anything else → <cwd>.
   73  // Returns "" for ScopeUser when no user dir is configured.
   74  func (s *Set) DocPath(scope Scope) string {
   75  	dir := s.CWD
   76  	names, def := docNames, defaultDocName
   77  	switch scope {
   78  	case ScopeUser:
   79  		if s.UserDir == "" {
   80  			return ""
   81  		}
   82  		dir = s.UserDir
   83  	case ScopeLocal:
   84  		names, def = localNames, defaultLocalName
   85  	}
   86  	for _, n := range names {
   87  		p := filepath.Join(dir, n)
   88  		if _, err := os.Stat(p); err == nil {
   89  			return p // append to the doc already in use
   90  		}
   91  	}
   92  	return filepath.Join(dir, def)
   93  }
   94
   95  // Empty reports whether the set carries nothing to inject, so Compose can leave
   96  // the base prompt byte-for-byte untouched (and the cache prefix maximal) when
   97  // there is no memory at all.
   98  func (s *Set) Empty() bool {
   99  	return s == nil || (len(s.Docs) == 0 && len(s.PinnedGuidance) == 0 && strings.TrimSpace(s.Index) == "")
  100  }
  101
  102  // docScopes are the scopes the panel can target for a quick-add or a new doc.
  103  // Ordered broad → specific for display.
  104  var docScopes = []Scope{ScopeUser, ScopeProject, ScopeLocal}
  105
  106  // allowedDocPaths is the closed set of files WriteDoc / AppendDoc may touch: the
  107  // canonical file for each writable scope, plus every doc already discovered this
  108  // session (so an ancestor or AGENTS.md the user is already editing stays
  109  // editable). Keyed by absolute path. This bounds frontend-driven writes to real
  110  // memory files rather than arbitrary paths.
  111  func (s *Set) allowedDocPaths() map[string]bool {
  112  	allow := map[string]bool{}
  113  	for _, sc := range docScopes {
  114  		if p := s.DocPath(sc); p != "" {
  115  			allow[absOf(p)] = true
  116  		}
  117  	}
  118  	for _, d := range s.Docs {
  119  		allow[absOf(d.Path)] = true
  120  	}
  121  	return allow
  122  }
  123
  124  // WriteDoc overwrites a doc-memory file with body, after checking path is a
04
DIMENSION · EXECUTION

执行环境与沙箱

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factexecution

01 · Bash 沙箱是独立于 permission 的 OS enforcement 层

源码事实

sandbox.Spec 把 Mode、WriteRoots、ForbidReadRoots、Network、MinimalWrites 和 Shell 分开；macOS 使用 Seatbelt，Linux 使用 bubblewrap，Windows 当前没有 OS-level Bash sandbox。要求 enforce 但 backend 不可用时拒绝运行未包装命令，而不是静默降级。

白话解释

审批是“允许不允许做”，沙箱是“允许做也只能在哪些目录/网络里做”；没有真正的后端时，默认宁可不跑。

对自研 Harness 的含义

不要把 Plan mode 或 permission ask 当沙箱；OS backend availability 应有显式 fail-closed 结果。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–6
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 7–14
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/sandbox/sandbox.go:1–14固定提交 ↗
    1  // Package sandbox wraps a shell command in an OS-level jail so the model's
    2  // `bash` calls are confined: it may read almost freely but write only inside
    3  // the writable roots (workspace, configured extras, plus temp and toolchain
    4  // caches), with optional forbid-read roots, and reach the network only when
    5  // allowed. This is the *enforcement* layer beneath the permission rules
    6  // (*policy*): a permitted command still cannot escape the box.
    7  //
    8  // macOS uses Seatbelt via sandbox-exec and Linux uses bubblewrap when available.
    9  // Windows does not currently provide an OS-level bash sandbox and resolves the
   10  // product setting to off. When enforce is requested but no OS sandbox backend
   11  // is available, the bash tool fails closed instead of running the command
   12  // unwrapped.
   13  // Confining the in-process file-writer built-ins is handled separately, in
   14  // package tool/builtin.
   15  package sandbox
   16
   17  import (
   18  	"runtime"
   19  )
   20
   21  // Spec describes how to confine one command. The zero value (Mode == "") does
   22  // not enforce, so an unconfigured caller runs commands unchanged.
   23  type Spec struct {
   24  	// Mode is "enforce" to wrap the command, anything else (incl. "off" and "")
   25  	// to run it unwrapped.
   26  	Mode string
   27  	// WriteRoots are directories the command may write to (the workspace root
   28  	// plus any configured extras). Platforms may add command-scoped temp/cache
   29  	// roots so builds and package managers keep working without broad writes.
   30  	WriteRoots []string
   31  	// ReadRoots are explicit host paths a Windows AppContainer may read. The
   32  	// macOS/Linux profiles already mount the host read-only by default.
   33  	ReadRoots []string
   34  	// AppContainerWriteRoots are the small subset of WriteRoots that a
   35  	// read-only Windows AppContainer may write (for MCP this is only its
   36  	// private state/temp tree). macOS and Linux already enforce this through
   37  	// WriteRoots and ignore this platform-specific distinction.
   38  	AppContainerWriteRoots []string
   39  	// DirectWrites marks a raw-argv launch as a write-capable command. On
   40  	// Windows this selects the low-integrity writer lane; it is deliberately
   41  	// false for ordinary read-only helpers such as rg.
   42  	DirectWrites bool
   43  	// ForbidReadRoots are files or directories the command may not read from
   44  	// when confined. The OS sandbox denies access to these paths (macOS Seatbelt
   45  	// deny file-read* rules, Linux bubblewrap masks); on other platforms the
   46  	// in-process tools enforce this instead.
   47  	ForbidReadRoots []string
   48  	// Network allows network egress from inside the sandbox. Off blocks it so a
   49  	// command cannot exfiltrate or fetch; many dev commands (module/package
   50  	// downloads) need it, so it defaults on at the config layer.
   51  	Network bool
   52  	// MinimalWrites omits the broad build-tool cache write allowances used by
   53  	// the bash sandbox. MCP profiles set it and explicitly provide only their
   54  	// private state/temp directories (plus approved writer roots).
   55  	MinimalWrites bool
   56  	// Shell is the interpreter the bash tool runs under. A zero value (empty
   57  	// Path) means the tool resolves one itself; the composition root sets it from
   58  	// [tools.shell] so the configured choice rides along with the spec.
   59  	Shell Shell
   60  }
   61
   62  // Enforce reports whether the spec asks for confinement.
   63  func (s Spec) Enforce() bool { return s.Mode == "enforce" }
   64
   65  // UnavailableMessage explains why an enforced bash sandbox cannot run and gives
   66  // the platform-specific remediation.
   67  func UnavailableMessage() string {
   68  	return "bash sandbox requested but unavailable on this host; refusing to run unconfined. " + UnavailableRemediation()
   69  }
   70
   71  // UnavailableRemediation is split out so status surfaces can append the same
   72  // actionable hint without repeating the leading error.
   73  func UnavailableRemediation() string {
   74  	switch runtime.GOOS {
   75  	case "linux":
   76  		return "Install bubblewrap (`bwrap`) or set [sandbox] bash = \"off\" in config.toml / Settings -> Sandbox to restore pre-1.16 unconfined shell execution."
   77  	case "darwin":
   78  		return "Ensure `sandbox-exec` is available on PATH or set [sandbox] bash = \"off\" in config.toml / Settings -> Sandbox to restore pre-1.16 unconfined shell execution."
   79  	case "windows":
   80  		return "Windows does not currently provide a Reasonix OS-level Bash sandbox; the effective setting is fixed to \"off\" and shell commands run unconfined."
   81  	default:
   82  		return "Set [sandbox] bash = \"off\" in config.toml / Settings -> Sandbox to run shell commands unconfined on this platform."
   83  	}
   84  }

02
L1factexecution

02 · 沙箱逃逸是单次、显式、可审计的二次授权

源码事实

EscapeApprover 只描述在 OS sandbox 启动失败后重跑一次 unconfined command，nil approver 代表 fail closed；boot 根据 workspace root、extra dirs、forbid read roots、network override 和 WorkspaceOnly 组装 bashSpec，并在 enforce 后端不可用时发出告警。

白话解释

沙箱坏了不自动打开裸 shell；只有带 UI 的宿主明确同意某一条命令，才允许这一次越界。

对自研 Harness 的含义

需要把“沙箱失败恢复”和“普通命令审批”拆成两个不同 approval kind，便于后台运行时安全默认拒绝。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 8–26
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 27–46
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/sandbox/escape.go:8–46固定提交 ↗
    8  // EscapeRequest describes a one-shot request to rerun a shell command without
    9  // the OS sandbox after the platform sandbox failed to start.
   10  type EscapeRequest struct {
   11  	Command string
   12  	Args    json.RawMessage
   13  	Reason  string
   14  }
   15
   16  // EscapeApprover asks the user whether one command may run unconfined after the
   17  // OS sandbox failed. Nil means fail closed.
   18  type EscapeApprover interface {
   19  	ApproveSandboxEscape(ctx context.Context, req EscapeRequest) (allow bool, reason string, err error)
   20  }
   21
   22  // EscapeSessionChecker reports whether a sandbox escape has already been
   23  // approved for the current session without prompting the user again.
   24  type EscapeSessionChecker interface {
   25  	SandboxEscapeSessionAllowed(ctx context.Context, req EscapeRequest) bool
   26  }
   27
   28  type escapeApproverContextKey struct{}
   29
   30  // WithEscapeApprover stamps an interactive sandbox-escape approver onto a tool
   31  // execution context.
   32  func WithEscapeApprover(ctx context.Context, approver EscapeApprover) context.Context {
   33  	if approver == nil {
   34  		return ctx
   35  	}
   36  	return context.WithValue(ctx, escapeApproverContextKey{}, approver)
   37  }
   38
   39  // EscapeApproverFrom returns the sandbox-escape approver carried by ctx.
   40  func EscapeApproverFrom(ctx context.Context) (EscapeApprover, bool) {
   41  	if ctx == nil {
   42  		return nil, false
   43  	}
   44  	approver, ok := ctx.Value(escapeApproverContextKey{}).(EscapeApprover)
   45  	return approver, ok && approver != nil
   46  }

05
DIMENSION · TOOLS

工具注册与执行

4 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1facttools

01 · 每个工具调用固定经过 parse→policy→prepare→finish 四阶段

源码事实

executeOne 为一个调用创建 toolCallPlan，先解析 canonical tool 并应用重复成功/失败和 stale-anchor guard，再做 Plan/proxy/delivery/recovery/permission，随后获取 lease/写 claim、运行 hooks 和 preview，最后 Execute、记录 receipt、运行 post hooks 并截断结果。

白话解释

模型只递交一张工单；真正执行前会先确认工具身份、是否允许、是否会改文件、是否拿到写锁和快照，执行后还要把回执写回账本。

对自研 Harness 的含义

工具扩展点要落在统一 pipeline 中，不能让某个 MCP 或别名工具绕过审批、锁、证据和输出预算。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 20–49
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 50–80
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。
