   20  // toolCallPlan holds the resolved, policy-checked state for one tool call.
   21  // Package-private; not shared across goroutines beyond the single executeOne
   22  // invocation that owns it.
   23  type toolCallPlan struct {
   24  	call          provider.ToolCall
   25  	tool          tool.Tool
   26  	canonicalName string
   27
   28  	permName     string
   29  	permArgs     json.RawMessage
   30  	execTool     tool.Tool
   31  	execArgs     json.RawMessage
   32  	evidenceName string
   33  	evidenceArgs json.RawMessage
   34  	readOnly     bool
   35
   36  	resolved     tool.ResolvedCall
   37  	resolvedMeta *tool.ResolvedCall
   38
   39  	mutates                   bool
   40  	verification              bool
   41  	planTransition            bool
   42  	planBefore                string
   43  	planAfter                 string
   44  	planReplacementAuthorized bool
   45  	recoveryGen               uint64
   46
   47  	runTool            tool.Tool
   48  	runArgs            json.RawMessage
   49  	cctx               context.Context
   50  	releaseParentWrite func()
   51  }
   52
   53  // executeOne runs a single tool call. It is pure with respect to the event sink
   54  // — the caller emits ToolDispatch/ToolResult — so it is safe to invoke from
   55  // parallel goroutines. Stages: parse → policy → prepare → finish.
   56  func (a *Agent) executeOne(ctx context.Context, call provider.ToolCall) (out toolOutcome) {
   57  	plan := &toolCallPlan{call: call}
   58  	defer func() {
   59  		if plan.releaseParentWrite != nil {
   60  			plan.releaseParentWrite()
   61  		}
   62  		if plan.resolvedMeta == nil {
   63  			return
   64  		}
   65  		out.resolved = true
   66  		out.resolvedName = plan.resolvedMeta.TargetName
   67  		out.capabilityID = plan.resolvedMeta.CapabilityID
   68  		out.resolvedReadOnly = plan.resolvedMeta.ReadOnly
   69  	}()
   70
   71  	if blocked, early := a.parseToolCall(plan); early {
   72  		return blocked
   73  	}
   74  	if blocked, early := a.resolveToolPolicy(ctx, plan); early {
   75  		return blocked
   76  	}
   77  	if blocked, early := a.prepareToolExecution(ctx, plan); early {
   78  		return blocked
   79  	}
   80  	return a.finishToolExecution(ctx, plan)
   81  }
   82
   83  // parseToolCall resolves the canonical tool, rejects ambiguity/unknown tools,
   84  // and applies repeat-success and stale-anchor guards.
   85  func (a *Agent) parseToolCall(plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
   86  	t, canonicalName, ambiguous := a.tools.ResolveCall(plan.call.Name)
   87  	if len(ambiguous) > 0 {
   88  		msg := fmt.Sprintf("ambiguous MCP tool reference %q; use one of: %s", plan.call.Name, strings.Join(ambiguous, ", "))
   89  		return toolOutcome{
   90  			output: "error: " + msg,
   91  			errMsg: msg,
   92  		}, true
   93  	}
   94  	if t == nil {
   95  		if server, ok := completedMCPConnect(a.tools, plan.call.Name); ok {
   96  			return toolOutcome{
   97  				output: fmt.Sprintf("MCP server %q is connected; its real tools are now available", server),
   98  			}, true
   99  		}
  100  		return toolOutcome{
  101  			output: fmt.Sprintf("error: unknown tool %q", plan.call.Name),
  102  			errMsg: fmt.Sprintf("unknown tool %q", plan.call.Name),
  103  		}, true
  104  	}
  105  	if out, blocked := a.repeatedSuccessBlock(plan.call, t); blocked {
  106  		return toolOutcome{
  107  			output:  out,
  108  			blocked: true,
  109  			errMsg:  loopGuardBlockErrMsg,
  110  		}, true
  111  	}
  112  	if out, blocked := a.repeatedFailureBlock(plan.call, t); blocked {
  113  		return toolOutcome{
  114  			output:  out,
  115  			blocked: true,
  116  			errMsg:  loopGuardBlockErrMsg,
  117  		}, true
  118  	}
  119  	if out, blocked := a.staleAnchorEditBlock(plan.call); blocked {
  120  		return toolOutcome{
  121  			output:  out,
  122  			blocked: true,
  123  			errMsg:  "blocked: fresh read required",
  124  		}, true
  125  	}
  126  	plan.tool = t
  127  	plan.canonicalName = canonicalName
  128  	plan.permName = canonicalName
  129  	plan.permArgs = json.RawMessage(plan.call.Arguments)
  130  	plan.execTool = t
  131  	plan.execArgs = json.RawMessage(plan.call.Arguments)
  132  	plan.evidenceName = canonicalName
  133  	plan.evidenceArgs = json.RawMessage(plan.call.Arguments)
  134  	plan.readOnly = t.ReadOnly()
  135  	return toolOutcome{}, false
  136  }
  137
  138  // resolveToolPolicy applies Plan mode, proxy resolution, delivery gates, Auto
  139  // Guard, and permission checks. Permission must complete before any write lease.
  140  func (a *Agent) resolveToolPolicy(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  141  	if blocked, early := a.applyPlanModeAndProxy(ctx, plan); early {
  142  		return blocked, true
  143  	}
  144  	if blocked, early := a.applyDeliveryPolicyGates(plan); early {
  145  		return blocked, true
  146  	}
  147  	if blocked, early := a.applyRecoveryAndPermission(ctx, plan); early {
  148  		return blocked, true
  149  	}
  150  	return toolOutcome{}, false
  151  }

internal/agent/execute_one.go · 9aaf8d381a21当前源码 ↗
   20
   21  // executeOne runs a single tool call. It is pure with respect to the event sink
   22  // — the caller emits ToolDispatch/ToolResult — so it is safe to invoke from
   23  // parallel goroutines. Stages: parse → policy → prepare → finish.
   24  func (a *Agent) executeOne(ctx context.Context, turn *turnRuntime, call provider.ToolCall) (out toolOutcome) {
   25  	ctx = a.withAgentContext(ctx)
   26  	plan := &toolCallPlan{call: call}
   27  	defer func() {
   28  		if plan.mutationObserved && !plan.mutationAfterDone {
   29  			a.observeAfterMutation(plan)
   30  		}
   31  		if plan.releaseMutationWrite != nil {
   32  			plan.releaseMutationWrite()
   33  		}
   34  		if plan.releaseParentWrite != nil {
   35  			plan.releaseParentWrite()
   36  		}
   37  		if plan.resolvedMeta == nil {
   38  			return
   39  		}
   40  		out.resolved = true
   41  		out.resolvedName = plan.resolvedMeta.TargetName
   42  		out.capabilityID = plan.resolvedMeta.CapabilityID
   43  		out.resolvedReadOnly = plan.resolvedMeta.ReadOnly
   44  	}()
   45  	defer finalizeWorkspaceMutationOutcome(&out, plan)
   46
   47  	if blocked, early := a.parseToolCall(ctx, plan); early {
   48  		return blocked
   49  	}
   50  	// tool.before: extensions rule on the parsed call before any policy or
   51  	// permission check. A valid replacement is re-parsed so every later stage
   52  	// sees the call that will actually execute.
   53  	if blocked, early := a.interceptToolBefore(ctx, plan); early {
   54  		return blocked
   55  	}
   56  	if blocked, early := a.resolveToolPolicy(ctx, turn, plan); early {
   57  		return blocked
   58  	}
   59  	if blocked, early := a.prepareToolExecution(ctx, plan); early {
   60  		return blocked
   61  	}
   62  	return a.finishToolExecution(ctx, plan)
   63  }
   64
   65  // parseToolCall resolves the canonical tool, rejects ambiguity/unknown tools,
   66  // and applies repeat-success and stale-anchor guards.
   67  func (a *Agent) parseToolCall(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
   68  	t, canonicalName, ambiguous := a.svc.tools.ResolveCall(plan.call.Name)
   69  	if len(ambiguous) > 0 {
   70  		msg := fmt.Sprintf("ambiguous MCP tool reference %q; use one of: %s", plan.call.Name, strings.Join(ambiguous, ", "))
   71  		return toolOutcome{
   72  			output: "error: " + msg,
   73  			errMsg: msg,
   74  		}, true
   75  	}
   76  	if t == nil {
   77  		if server, ok := completedMCPConnect(a.svc.tools, plan.call.Name); ok {
   78  			return toolOutcome{
   79  				output: fmt.Sprintf("MCP server %q is connected; its real tools are now available", server),
   80  			}, true
   81  		}
   82  		return toolOutcome{
   83  			output: fmt.Sprintf("error: unknown tool %q", plan.call.Name),
   84  			errMsg: fmt.Sprintf("unknown tool %q", plan.call.Name),
   85  		}, true
   86  	}
   87  	if out, blocked := a.repeatedSuccessBlock(plan.call, t); blocked {
   88  		return toolOutcome{
   89  			output:  out,
   90  			blocked: true,
   91  			errMsg:  loopGuardBlockErrMsg,
   92  		}, true
   93  	}
   94  	if out, blocked := a.repeatedFailureBlock(ctx, plan.call, t); blocked {
   95  		return toolOutcome{
   96  			output:  out,
   97  			blocked: true,
   98  			errMsg:  loopGuardBlockErrMsg,
   99  		}, true
  100  	}
  101  	if out, blocked := a.staleAnchorEditBlock(plan.call); blocked {
  102  		return toolOutcome{
  103  			output:  out,
  104  			blocked: true,
  105  			errMsg:  "blocked: fresh read required",
  106  		}, true
  107  	}
  108  	plan.tool = t
  109  	plan.canonicalName = canonicalName
  110  	plan.permName = canonicalName
  111  	plan.permArgs = json.RawMessage(plan.call.Arguments)
  112  	plan.execTool = t
  113  	plan.execArgs = json.RawMessage(plan.call.Arguments)
  114  	plan.evidenceName = canonicalName
  115  	plan.evidenceArgs = json.RawMessage(plan.call.Arguments)
  116  	plan.readOnly = t.ReadOnly()
  117  	if canonicalName == "bash" {
  118  		var permissionReader bool
  119  		plan.effects, permissionReader = evidence.ClassifyBashToolCall(plan.execArgs)
  120  		if permissionReader {
  121  			// Bash is schema-level writer-capable, but the host can resolve a
  122  			// concrete invocation to read-only after parsing its arguments. Carry
  123  			// that fact through permission, mutation accounting, evidence, and the
  124  			// refreshed local tool receipt without changing the provider schema.
  125  			plan.readOnly = true
  126  			plan.resolvedMeta = &tool.ResolvedCall{TargetName: canonicalName, ReadOnly: true}
  127  		}
  128  	} else {
  129  		plan.effects = evidence.ClassifyToolCall(plan.evidenceName, plan.evidenceArgs, plan.readOnly)
  130  	}
  131  	return toolOutcome{}, false
  132  }
  133
  134  // resolveToolPolicy applies Plan mode, proxy resolution, delivery gates, Auto
  135  // Guard, and permission checks. Permission must complete before any write lease.
  136  func (a *Agent) resolveToolPolicy(ctx context.Context, turn *turnRuntime, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  137  	if blocked, early := a.applyPlanModeAndProxy(ctx, plan); early {
  138  		return blocked, true
  139  	}
  140  	if blocked, early := a.applyContextualToolGate(ctx, plan); early {
  141  		return blocked, true
  142  	}
  143  	if blocked, early := a.applyDeliveryPolicyGates(turn, plan); early {
  144  		return blocked, true
  145  	}
  146  	// After proxy resolution, re-apply the batch mutation barrier using the
  147  	// real target classification. Provider-visible proxies such as
  148  	// use_capability advertise ReadOnly()==true before resolution and would
  149  	// otherwise slip past the pre-run skip pass.
  150  	if blocked, early := a.applyMutationDependencyBarrier(plan); early {
  151  		return blocked, true
02
L1facttools

02 · use_capability 代理会先解析真实 MCP 目标，再重新做 Plan 与安全判断

源码事实

proxy resolver 在权限、hooks 和 evidence 之前把 provider-visible call 映射到真实 target/name/args；解析后的目标会再次检查 Plan safety，Plan 中只有已授权、非 destructive 的 MCP 可走 planner trusted path，普通 writer 或未授权 MCP 被阻断。

白话解释

模型看到的是一个稳定的“能力入口”，但系统不会因为套了一层代理就放过真实目标；拆包后还要重新验一次。

对自研 Harness 的含义

统一 capability proxy 能保持 prompt schema 稳定，但必须做 resolution 后的第二次安全判定和审计双回执。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 153–210
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 211–269
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/agent/execute_one.go:153–269固定提交 ↗
  153  // applyPlanModeAndProxy handles initial Plan mode, proxy resolution / skip path,
  154  // resolved-target Plan re-check, and MCP Plan availability.
  155  func (a *Agent) applyPlanModeAndProxy(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  156  	t := plan.tool
  157  	call := plan.call
  158  	if a.planMode.Load() {
  159  		// Translate the tool's optional plan-mode self-report into the policy's
  160  		// tri-state. Mirrors the t.(tool.Previewer) assertion precedent below.
  161  		safety := planmode.PlanSafetyUnknown
  162  		if c, ok := t.(tool.PlanModeClassifier); ok {
  163  			if c.PlanModeSafe() {
  164  				safety = planmode.PlanSafetySafe
  165  			} else {
  166  				safety = planmode.PlanSafetyUnsafe
  167  			}
  168  		}
  169  		if decision := a.planModeDecision(plan.canonicalName, t.ReadOnly(), safety, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments)); decision.Blocked {
  170  			return toolOutcome{
  171  				output:  decision.Message,
  172  				blocked: true,
  173  				errMsg:  "blocked: tool is unavailable during planning",
  174  			}, true
  175  		}
  176  	}
  177  	// Resolve proxy tools (use_capability) to the real MCP target before
  178  	// permission, hooks, and evidence. Provider transcript keeps call.Name.
  179  	if resolver, ok := t.(tool.CallResolver); ok {
  180  		rc, rerr := resolver.ResolveCall(ctx, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments))
  181  		if rerr != nil {
  182  			return toolOutcome{
  183  				output: fmt.Sprintf("error: %v", rerr),
  184  				errMsg: firstLine(rerr.Error()),
  185  			}, true
  186  		}
  187  		plan.resolved = rc
  188  		plan.resolvedMeta = &plan.resolved
  189  		if rc.TargetName != "" {
  190  			plan.permName = rc.TargetName
  191  			plan.evidenceName = rc.TargetName
  192  		}
  193  		if len(rc.Args) > 0 {
  194  			plan.permArgs = rc.Args
  195  			plan.evidenceArgs = rc.Args
  196  			plan.execArgs = rc.Args
  197  		}
  198  		if rc.Target != nil {
  199  			plan.execTool = rc.Target
  200  		}
  201  		plan.readOnly = rc.ReadOnly
  202  		if outcome, blocked := a.readOnlyExecutionBlock(t, &rc); blocked {
  203  			return outcome, true
  204  		}
  205  		if rc.Commit != nil {
  206  			if err := rc.Commit(); err != nil {
  207  				return toolOutcome{
  208  					output: fmt.Sprintf("error: %v", err),
  209  					errMsg: firstLine(err.Error()),
  210  				}, true
  211  			}
  212  		}
  213  		if rc.SkipExecute {
  214  			// Resolution completed without target execution; still record a meta receipt.
  215  			// A connected mcp-server call completes during resolution by listing
  216  			// its live tools, so account for that successful call here too.
  217  			if rc.ProxyAction == "call" && !rc.Unavailable {
  218  				a.noteCapabilityInvocation(call.Name, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments), nil)
  219  			}
  220  			result := rc.Result
  221  			if a.evidence != nil {
  222  				// inspect/decline are not mutations; unavailable call targets are not success.
  223  				success := !rc.Unavailable
  224  				rec := evidence.ReceiptFromToolCall(call.Name, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments), success, true)
  225  				a.evidence.Record(rec)
  226  			}
  227  			if rc.Unavailable {
  228  				return toolOutcome{output: result, errMsg: firstLine(rc.UnavailableReason)}, true
  229  			}
  230  			body, truncMsg := truncateToolOutput(result)
  231  			return toolOutcome{output: body, truncated: truncMsg != "", truncMsg: truncMsg}, true
  232  		}
  233  	} else if outcome, blocked := a.readOnlyExecutionBlock(t, nil); blocked {
  234  		return outcome, true
  235  	}
  236
  237  	// A proxy resolution can point at a target with an explicit planning-phase
  238  	// opt-out even though the proxy itself has none. Re-check the resolved target
  239  	// before its ordinary permission and sandbox path.
  240  	if plan.resolved.TargetName != "" && a.planMode.Load() {
  241  		safety := planmode.PlanSafetyUnknown
  242  		if c, ok := plan.execTool.(tool.PlanModeClassifier); ok {
  243  			if c.PlanModeSafe() {
  244  				safety = planmode.PlanSafetySafe
  245  			} else {
  246  				safety = planmode.PlanSafetyUnsafe
  247  			}
  248  		}
  249  		if decision := a.planModeDecision(plan.permName, plan.resolved.ReadOnly, safety, plan.permArgs); decision.Blocked {
  250  			return toolOutcome{
  251  				output:  decision.Message,
  252  				blocked: true,
  253  				errMsg:  "blocked: tool is unavailable during planning",
  254  			}, true
  255  		}
  256  	}
  257  	plannerTrustedMCP := a.plannerMCPExecution && isMCPExecutionTarget(plan.execTool, plan.permName) && mcpServerAuthorized(plan.execTool) && !mcpDestructiveHint(plan.execTool)
  258  	if a.planMode.Load() && isMCPExecutionTarget(plan.execTool, plan.permName) && !plannerTrustedMCP && (!plan.readOnly || !mcpServerAuthorized(plan.execTool) || mcpDestructiveHint(plan.execTool)) {
  259  		reason := "writer/destructive target"
  260  		if plan.readOnly && !mcpServerAuthorized(plan.execTool) {
  261  			reason = "reader from an unauthorized server"
  262  		}
  263  		return toolOutcome{
  264  			output:  fmt.Sprintf("blocked: MCP %s %q is unavailable during Plan mode; finish or exit Plan mode before requesting this call", reason, plan.permName),
  265  			blocked: true,
  266  			errMsg:  "blocked: MCP target is unavailable during planning",
  267  		}, true
  268  	}
  269  	return toolOutcome{}, false
  270  }
  271
  272  // applyDeliveryPolicyGates enforces delivery-profile bash and criteria rules and
  273  // classifies whether the call mutates workspace state.
  274  func (a *Agent) applyDeliveryPolicyGates(plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  275  	if a.deliveryProfile && plan.evidenceName == "bash" && evidence.BashToolCallMasksVerificationExit(plan.evidenceArgs) {
  276  		return toolOutcome{
  277  			output:  "blocked: the trailing echo/printf of $? masks the verifier's exit status, so this command would look successful even when the check failed. Run the verifier or read-only extraction pipeline by itself and let its exit status be the tool result; for example: tail ... | head ... | node --check -",
  278  			blocked: true,
  279  			errMsg:  "blocked: verification exit status masked",
  280  		}, true
  281  	}
  282  	if a.deliveryProfile && plan.evidenceName == "bash" && evidence.BashToolCallMixesMutationAndVerification(plan.evidenceArgs) {
  283  		return toolOutcome{
  284  			output:  "blocked: this command mixes a verification check with a segment that may write state. Run the state-changing preparation separately while a todo is in_progress, then run a read-only verification command. For generated input, prefer a host-recognized read-only pipeline into the verifier (for example: tail ... | head ... | node --check -) instead of writing a temporary file.",
  285  			blocked: true,
  286  			errMsg:  "blocked: mixed mutation and verification command",
  287  		}, true
  288  	}
  289  	if a.deliveryProfile && plan.evidenceName == "bash" && evidence.BashToolCallUsesOpaqueInlineInterpreter(plan.evidenceArgs) {
  290  		return toolOutcome{
  291  			output:  "blocked: delivery mode cannot audit inline interpreter source such as node -e or python -c, so executing it would become an opaque mutation and invalidate prior verification. For inspection, use read_file/grep or another host-proven read-only command. For validation, use a conventional verifier such as node --check, a project test/check/lint command, or a read-only extraction pipeline into the verifier. For an intentional state change, use a file tool or a script file under the current in_progress todo. " + evidence.VerificationCommandSummary(),
  292  			blocked: true,
  293  			errMsg:  "blocked: opaque inline interpreter command",
  294  		}, true
  295  	}
  296
  297  	plan.mutates = evidence.ToolCallMutates(plan.evidenceName, plan.evidenceArgs, plan.readOnly)
  298  	if a.deliveryProfile && evidence.ToolCallRequiresDeliveryCriteria(plan.evidenceName, plan.evidenceArgs, plan.readOnly) && !a.deliveryCriteriaEstablished {
  299  		return toolOutcome{
  300  			output:  "blocked: delivery-first mode requires acceptance criteria before state-changing work. Call todo_write with a concrete, verifiable task list, then retry this tool call.",
  301  			blocked: true,
  302  			errMsg:  "blocked: delivery acceptance criteria required",
  303  		}, true
  304  	}
  305  	if a.deliveryProfile && plan.mutates && !a.hasActiveCanonicalTodo() {
  306  		return toolOutcome{
  307  			output:  "blocked: delivery-first mode requires every state change to belong to the current in_progress todo. Preserve the completed todo prefix, append a concrete new item if more work was discovered, mark that item in_progress with todo_write, then retry this mutation.",
  308  			blocked: true,
  309  			errMsg:  "blocked: active delivery todo required",
  310  		}, true
  311  	}
  312  	return toolOutcome{}, false
  313  }
  314
  315  // applyRecoveryAndPermission runs Auto Guard then ordinary permission. Neither
  316  // acquires a write lease; that happens only after permission in prepare.
  317  func (a *Agent) applyRecoveryAndPermission(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  318  	// Auto Guard: after resolution/mutation classification, before
  319  	// permission approval and workspace write-lock acquisition, so a waiting
  320  	// recovery card never holds a write lease. Consult on mutations,
  321  	// verification, plan transitions, and again for every tool once an Episode
  322  	// is exhausted so host-proven read-only diagnosis can remain available while
  323  	// further execution is quarantined. Ask/Yolo still bypass inside the gate.
  324  	plan.verification = plan.evidenceName == "bash" && evidence.IsDeliveryVerificationCommand(bashCommandFromArgs(plan.evidenceArgs))
  325  	plan.planTransition, plan.planBefore, plan.planAfter = a.recoveryPlanTransition(plan.evidenceName, plan.evidenceArgs)
  326  	episodeStopped := false
  327  	if ctrl := a.recoveryEpisodeControl(); ctrl != nil {
  328  		plan.recoveryGen = ctrl.Generation()
  329  		episodeStopped = ctrl.EpisodeStopped(a.recoveryTaskID)
  330  	}
  331  	if a.recoveryGate != nil && (plan.mutates || plan.verification || plan.planTransition || episodeStopped) {
  332  		subject := recoverySubject(plan.evidenceName, plan.evidenceArgs)
  333  		if plan.planTransition {
  334  			subject = "Update the active execution plan"
  335  		}
  336  		preview := strings.TrimSpace(plan.call.Diff)
  337  		if preview == "" {
  338  			preview = subject
  339  		}
  340  		if plan.planTransition {

  159  func (a *Agent) applyContextualToolGate(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  160  	if plan == nil || plan.tool == nil {
  161  		return toolOutcome{}, false
  162  	}
  163  	if outcome, blocked := contextualToolGateOutcome(ctx, plan.tool, plan.canonicalName); blocked {
  164  		return outcome, true
  165  	}
  166  	if plan.execTool != nil {
  167  		if outcome, blocked := contextualToolGateOutcome(ctx, plan.execTool, plan.permName); blocked {
  168  			return outcome, true
  169  		}
  170  	}
  171  	return toolOutcome{}, false
  172  }
  173
  174  func contextualToolGateOutcome(ctx context.Context, target tool.Tool, name string) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  175  	contextual, ok := target.(tool.ContextualTool)
  176  	if !ok || contextual.ProviderVisible(ctx) {
  177  		return toolOutcome{}, false
  178  	}
  179  	msg := fmt.Sprintf("blocked: tool %q is unavailable in the current workflow context", name)
  180  	switch name {
  181  	case "update_goal":
  182  		msg = "update_goal is only available while an active goal turn is running — no goal state was changed"
  183  	case "complete_step":
  184  		msg = "blocked: complete_step is only available after plan approval. While planning, keep task state with todo_write and present the plan for user approval."
  185  	case "bash_output", "wait", "kill_shell":
  186  		msg = "background jobs are not available in this context"
  187  	}
  188  	return toolOutcome{output: msg, blocked: true, errMsg: firstLine(msg)}, true
  189  }
  190
  191  // applyMutationDependencyBarrier blocks later mutations and verifications in the
  192  // same provider batch after an earlier modification failed. Host-proven
  193  // read-only diagnosis (resolved ReadOnly with no verification classification)
  194  // still runs.
  195  func (a *Agent) applyMutationDependencyBarrier(plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  196  	if a == nil || plan == nil {
  197  		return toolOutcome{}, false
  198  	}
  199  	cause := a.mutationDependencyBarrier.Load()
  200  	if cause == nil {
  201  		return toolOutcome{}, false
  202  	}
  203  	verification := plan.evidenceName == "bash" && evidence.IsDeliveryVerificationCommand(bashCommandFromArgs(plan.evidenceArgs))
  204  	if !plan.effects.StateMutation && !verification {
  205  		return toolOutcome{}, false
  206  	}
  207  	msg := cause.message()
  208  	var ex *tool.ShellExecution
  209  	// Structured shell metadata only for bash cards; other tools keep plain text.
  210  	if plan.evidenceName == "bash" || plan.call.Name == "bash" {
  211  		ex = shellPreflightExecution(plan, verification)
  212  		if ex != nil {
  213  			ex.FailurePhase = tool.ShellPhaseDependency
  214  			ex.State = tool.ShellStateNotRun
  215  			ex.MutationRisk = tool.ShellMutationNotStarted
  216  			if verification {
  217  				ex.Verification = tool.ShellVerificationNotRun
  218  			}
  219  		}
  220  	}
  221  	return toolOutcome{
  222  		output:    msg,
  223  		blocked:   true,
  224  		errMsg:    firstLine(msg),
  225  		execution: ex,
  226  	}, true
  227  }

  229  // applyPlanModeAndProxy handles initial Plan mode, proxy resolution / skip path,
  230  // resolved-target Plan re-check, and MCP Plan availability.
  231  func (a *Agent) applyPlanModeAndProxy(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  232  	t := plan.tool
  233  	call := plan.call
  234  	if a.planMode.Load() {
  235  		// Translate the tool's optional plan-mode self-report into the policy's
  236  		// tri-state. Mirrors the t.(tool.Previewer) assertion precedent below.
  237  		safety := planmode.PlanSafetyUnknown
  238  		if c, ok := t.(tool.PlanModeClassifier); ok {
  239  			if c.PlanModeSafe() {
  240  				safety = planmode.PlanSafetySafe
  241  			} else {
  242  				safety = planmode.PlanSafetyUnsafe
  243  			}
  244  		}
  245  		if decision := a.planModeDecision(plan.canonicalName, t.ReadOnly(), safety, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments)); decision.Blocked {
  246  			return toolOutcome{
  247  				output:  decision.Message,
  248  				blocked: true,
  249  				errMsg:  "blocked: tool is unavailable during planning",
  250  			}, true
  251  		}
  252  	}
  253  	// Resolve proxy tools (use_capability) to the real MCP target before
  254  	// permission, hooks, and evidence. Provider transcript keeps call.Name.
  255  	if resolver, ok := t.(tool.CallResolver); ok {
  256  		rc, rerr := resolver.ResolveCall(ctx, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments))
  257  		if rerr != nil {
  258  			return toolOutcome{
  259  				output: fmt.Sprintf("error: %v", rerr),
  260  				errMsg: firstLine(rerr.Error()),
  261  			}, true
  262  		}
  263  		plan.resolved = rc
  264  		plan.resolvedMeta = &plan.resolved
  265  		if rc.TargetName != "" {
  266  			plan.permName = rc.TargetName
  267  			plan.evidenceName = rc.TargetName
  268  		}
  269  		if len(rc.Args) > 0 {
  270  			plan.permArgs = rc.Args
  271  			plan.evidenceArgs = rc.Args
  272  			plan.execArgs = rc.Args
  273  		}
  274  		if rc.Target != nil {
  275  			plan.execTool = rc.Target
  276  		}
  277  		if outcome, blocked := contextualToolGateOutcome(ctx, plan.execTool, plan.permName); blocked {
  278  			return outcome, true
  279  		}
  280  		plan.readOnly = rc.ReadOnly
  281  		plan.classifyEffects()
  282  		if outcome, blocked := a.readOnlyExecutionBlock(t, &rc); blocked {
  283  			return outcome, true
  284  		}
  285  		if rc.Commit != nil {
  286  			if err := rc.Commit(); err != nil {
  287  				return toolOutcome{
  288  					output: fmt.Sprintf("error: %v", err),
  289  					errMsg: firstLine(err.Error()),
  290  				}, true
  291  			}
  292  		}
  293  		if rc.SkipExecute {
  294  			// Resolution completed without target execution; still record a meta receipt.
  295  			// A connected mcp-server call completes during resolution by listing
  296  			// its live tools, so account for that successful call here too.
  297  			if rc.ProxyAction == "call" && !rc.Unavailable {
  298  				a.noteCapabilityInvocation(call.Name, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments), nil)
  299  			}
  300  			result := rc.Result
  301  			if a.task.ledger != nil {
  302  				// inspect/decline are not mutations; unavailable call targets are not success.
  303  				success := !rc.Unavailable
  304  				rec := evidence.ReceiptFromToolCall(call.Name, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments), success, true)
  305  				a.task.ledger.Record(rec)
  306  			}
  307  			if rc.Unavailable {
  308  				return toolOutcome{output: result, errMsg: firstLine(rc.UnavailableReason)}, true
  309  			}
  310  			body, truncMsg := truncateToolOutputFor(result, plan.call.Name, plan.call.ID)
  311  			out := toolOutcome{output: body, truncated: truncMsg != "", truncMsg: truncMsg}
  312  			if truncMsg != "" {
  313  				out.rawOutput = result
  314  			}
  315  			return out, true
  316  		}
  317  	} else if outcome, blocked := a.readOnlyExecutionBlock(t, nil); blocked {
  318  		return outcome, true
  319  	}
  320
  321  	// A proxy resolution can point at a target with an explicit planning-phase
  322  	// opt-out even though the proxy itself has none. Re-check the resolved target
  323  	// before its ordinary permission and sandbox path.
  324  	if plan.resolved.TargetName != "" && a.planMode.Load() {
  325  		safety := planmode.PlanSafetyUnknown
  326  		if c, ok := plan.execTool.(tool.PlanModeClassifier); ok {
  327  			if c.PlanModeSafe() {
  328  				safety = planmode.PlanSafetySafe
  329  			} else {
  330  				safety = planmode.PlanSafetyUnsafe
  331  			}
  332  		}
  333  		if decision := a.planModeDecision(plan.permName, plan.resolved.ReadOnly, safety, plan.permArgs); decision.Blocked {
  334  			return toolOutcome{
  335  				output:  decision.Message,
  336  				blocked: true,
  337  				errMsg:  "blocked: tool is unavailable during planning",
  338  			}, true
  339  		}
  340  	}
03
L1facttools

03 · Delivery 模式把验收标准变成 host-enforced tool policy

源码事实

Delivery gates 拒绝会遮蔽 verifier exit code、混合 mutation+verification、opaque inline interpreter 的 Bash；state-changing call 之前必须完成 todo_write 并有 active canonical todo。permission 和 Auto Guard 通过后才获取 workspace write lease，hooks/preview 仍在 Execute 前发生。

白话解释

交付模式不接受“我顺便跑了个检查并 echo $?”这种无法审计的成功；先列验收清单，改完后单独验证，再签收。

对自研 Harness 的含义

这是一种把“完成定义”放入执行器的强约束，比只在 system prompt 里提醒可靠。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 272–291
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 292–312
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  298  	if a.deliveryProfile && evidence.ToolCallRequiresDeliveryCriteria(plan.evidenceName, plan.evidenceArgs, plan.readOnly) && !a.deliveryCriteriaEstablished {
  299  		return toolOutcome{
  300  			output:  "blocked: delivery-first mode requires acceptance criteria before state-changing work. Call todo_write with a concrete, verifiable task list, then retry this tool call.",
  301  			blocked: true,
  302  			errMsg:  "blocked: delivery acceptance criteria required",
  303  		}, true
  304  	}
  305  	if a.deliveryProfile && plan.mutates && !a.hasActiveCanonicalTodo() {
  306  		return toolOutcome{
  307  			output:  "blocked: delivery-first mode requires every state change to belong to the current in_progress todo. Preserve the completed todo prefix, append a concrete new item if more work was discovered, mark that item in_progress with todo_write, then retry this mutation.",
  308  			blocked: true,
  309  			errMsg:  "blocked: active delivery todo required",
  310  		}, true
  311  	}
  312  	return toolOutcome{}, false
  313  }
  314
  315  // applyRecoveryAndPermission runs Auto Guard then ordinary permission. Neither
  316  // acquires a write lease; that happens only after permission in prepare.
  317  func (a *Agent) applyRecoveryAndPermission(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  318  	// Auto Guard: after resolution/mutation classification, before
  319  	// permission approval and workspace write-lock acquisition, so a waiting
  320  	// recovery card never holds a write lease. Consult on mutations,
  321  	// verification, plan transitions, and again for every tool once an Episode
  322  	// is exhausted so host-proven read-only diagnosis can remain available while
  323  	// further execution is quarantined. Ask/Yolo still bypass inside the gate.
  324  	plan.verification = plan.evidenceName == "bash" && evidence.IsDeliveryVerificationCommand(bashCommandFromArgs(plan.evidenceArgs))
  325  	plan.planTransition, plan.planBefore, plan.planAfter = a.recoveryPlanTransition(plan.evidenceName, plan.evidenceArgs)
  326  	episodeStopped := false
  327  	if ctrl := a.recoveryEpisodeControl(); ctrl != nil {
  328  		plan.recoveryGen = ctrl.Generation()
  329  		episodeStopped = ctrl.EpisodeStopped(a.recoveryTaskID)
  330  	}
  331  	if a.recoveryGate != nil && (plan.mutates || plan.verification || plan.planTransition || episodeStopped) {
  332  		subject := recoverySubject(plan.evidenceName, plan.evidenceArgs)
  333  		if plan.planTransition {
  334  			subject = "Update the active execution plan"
  335  		}
  336  		preview := strings.TrimSpace(plan.call.Diff)
  337  		if preview == "" {
  338  			preview = subject
  339  		}
  340  		if plan.planTransition {
  341  			preview = plan.planAfter
  342  		}
  343  		episodeID := ""
  344  		if ctrl := a.recoveryEpisodeControl(); ctrl != nil {
  345  			episodeID = ctrl.EpisodeID()
  346  		}
  347  		dec, rerr := a.recoveryGate.BeforeMutation(ctx, RecoveryProposal{
  348  			AgentID:        a.recoveryAgentID,
  349  			TaskID:         a.recoveryTaskID,
  350  			TaskScopeID:    recoveryTaskScopeID(a.deliveryScopeID, a.recoveryRunSeq.Load()),
  351  			EpisodeID:      episodeID,
  352  			TaskSummary:    a.recoveryTaskSummary,
  353  			Tool:           plan.evidenceName,
  354  			Args:           plan.evidenceArgs,
  355  			Subject:        subject,
  356  			Preview:        preview,
  357  			ReadOnly:       plan.readOnly,
  358  			Mutates:        plan.mutates,
  359  			Verification:   plan.verification,
  360  			PlanTransition: plan.planTransition,
  361  			PlanBefore:     plan.planBefore,
  362  			PlanAfter:      plan.planAfter,
  363  		})
  364  		if dec.Generation != 0 {
  365  			plan.recoveryGen = dec.Generation
  366  		}
  367  		if rerr != nil && !dec.Blocked {
  368  			return toolOutcome{
  369  				output:             fmt.Sprintf("blocked: Auto Guard error: %v", rerr),
  370  				blocked:            true,
  371  				errMsg:             "blocked: Auto Guard error",
  372  				recoveryGeneration: plan.recoveryGen,
  373  			}, true
  374  		}
  375  		if dec.Blocked || !dec.Allow {
  376  			msg := strings.TrimSpace(dec.Message)
  377  			if msg == "" {
  378  				msg = "blocked: Auto Guard declined this mutation"
  379  			}
  380  			if !strings.HasPrefix(msg, "blocked:") {
  381  				msg = "blocked: " + msg
  382  			}
  383  			return toolOutcome{

  356  // applyDeliveryPolicyGates enforces global deterministic shell contracts plus
  357  // delivery-profile-only criteria rules, and classifies mutation/verification.
  358  func (a *Agent) applyDeliveryPolicyGates(turn *turnRuntime, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  359  	// Global deterministic shell contract (ordinary + Delivery). PowerShell 5.1
  360  	// &&/|| is enforced inside the bash tool itself so descriptor and error text
  361  	// stay shell-accurate; the agent layers apply command-shape protections.
  362  	// Delivery keeps its longer recovery copy so existing delivery guidance tests
  363  	// and model recovery prompts stay stable.
  364  	//
  365  	// Ordinary mode blocks only shapes where a later segment can actually hide an
  366  	// earlier failure. Delivery keeps the broader classifier because a mutation
  367  	// invalidates the verification receipt even when the exit status is honest.
  368  	// Without that split, `go build ./... && go test ./...`, `npm install &&
  369  	// npm test`, and every other short-circuit chain would be rejected for every
  370  	// user, though bash already reports the failing step's status for them.
  371  	if plan.evidenceName == "bash" {
  372  		if evidence.BashToolCallMasksVerificationExit(plan.evidenceArgs) {
  373  			msg := evidence.ShellContractPreflightMessage("mask_exit")
  374  			if a.deliveryProfile {
  375  				msg = "blocked: the trailing echo/printf of $? masks the verifier's exit status, so this command would look successful even when the check failed. Run the verifier or read-only extraction pipeline by itself and let its exit status be the tool result; for example: tail ... | head ... | node --check -"
  376  			}
  377  			return toolOutcome{
  378  				output:    msg,
  379  				blocked:   true,
  380  				errMsg:    "blocked: verification exit status masked",
  381  				execution: shellPreflightExecution(plan, true),
  382  			}, true
  383  		}
04
L1facttools

04 · 执行结果会同时写证据账本、hooks 和恢复观测

源码事实

finishToolExecution 在真实 target 执行后写入 model-visible receipt 和 proxy target receipt，记录 output bytes/todo 状态，触发 PostToolUse 或 failure hook、Auto Guard observation，再统一截断回模型的输出。

白话解释

工具成功不等于只把一段字符串塞回聊天：系统还保存“谁调用了谁、是否真的产出、哪个 todo 前进了、恢复守卫看到什么”。

对自研 Harness 的含义

审计与恢复需要结构化 receipt，而不是从自然语言最终答案里猜发生过什么。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 552–602
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 603–654
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/agent/execute_one.go:552–654固定提交 ↗
  552  // finishToolExecution performs the concrete Execute, records evidence, runs
  553  // post hooks and recovery observation, and truncates the model-facing result.
  554  func (a *Agent) finishToolExecution(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) toolOutcome {
  555  	cctx := plan.cctx
  556  	runTool := plan.runTool
  557  	runArgs := plan.runArgs
  558  	call := plan.call
  559  	t := plan.tool
  560  	readOnly := plan.readOnly
  561  	permName := plan.permName
  562  	permArgs := plan.permArgs
  563  	evidenceName := plan.evidenceName
  564  	evidenceArgs := plan.evidenceArgs
  565  	mutates := plan.mutates
  566  	recoveryGen := plan.recoveryGen
  567
  568  	var result string
  569  	var images []string
  570  	var err error
  571  	// A call that was authorized under reader classification carries that
  572  	// basis into dispatch: the MCP execution layer re-verifies it linearizably
  573  	// against server authorization and live safety metadata, and refuses to
  574  	// promote it into a writer lane if reclassification landed after the gate.
  575  	if readOnly && isInstalledMCPTool(runTool) && mcpServerAuthorized(runTool) && !mcpDestructiveHint(runTool) {
  576  		cctx = tool.WithReaderExecutionIntent(cctx)
  577  	}
  578  	// Planner-trusted MCP: authorized + non-destructive, even without
  579  	// readOnlyHint. Final dispatch re-checks live authorization/destructiveHint.
  580  	if a.plannerMCPExecution && isMCPExecutionTarget(runTool, permName) && mcpServerAuthorized(runTool) && !mcpDestructiveHint(runTool) {
  581  		cctx = tool.WithNonDestructiveMCPExecutionIntent(cctx)
  582  	}
  583  	if it, ok := runTool.(tool.ImageTool); ok {
  584  		result, images, err = it.ExecuteWithImages(cctx, runArgs)
  585  	} else {
  586  		result, err = runTool.Execute(cctx, runArgs)
  587  	}
  588  	if a.evidence != nil {
  589  		// Always record the model-visible call for audit, then the real target
  590  		// attributes for mutation/read classification when they differ.
  591  		if call.Name == "complete_step" {
  592  			rec := evidence.ReceiptFromToolCall(call.Name, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments), err == nil, t.ReadOnly())
  593  			a.evidence.Record(rec)
  594  			if err == nil {
  595  				a.advanceCanonicalTodo(rec.Step)
  596  			}
  597  		} else if evidenceName != call.Name {
  598  			// Proxy: meta receipt (non-mutation) + real target receipt.
  599  			a.evidence.Record(evidence.ReceiptFromToolCall(call.Name, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments), err == nil, true))
  600  			rec := evidence.ReceiptFromToolCall(evidenceName, evidenceArgs, err == nil, readOnly)
  601  			rec.OutputBytes = len(strings.TrimSpace(result))
  602  			a.evidence.Record(rec)
  603  		} else {
  604  			rec := evidence.ReceiptFromToolCall(call.Name, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments), err == nil, t.ReadOnly())
  605  			rec.OutputBytes = len(strings.TrimSpace(result))
  606  			a.evidence.Record(rec)
  607  			if err == nil && call.Name == "todo_write" {
  608  				a.setTodoState(rec.Todos)
  609  				if len(rec.Todos) > 0 {
  610  					a.deliveryCriteriaEstablished = true
  611  				}
  612  			}
  613  		}
  614  	}
  615  	// Track skill/capability outcomes for Delivery gates.
  616  	a.noteCapabilityInvocation(call.Name, json.RawMessage(call.Arguments), err)
  617  	// Success and failure hooks observe the result after the tool ran. Use the
  618  	// real target name for proxied tools.
  619  	if a.hooks != nil {
  620  		if err != nil {
  621  			a.hooks.PostToolUseFailure(ctx, permName, permArgs, result, err)
  622  		} else {
  623  			a.hooks.PostToolUse(ctx, permName, permArgs, result)
  624  		}
  625  	}
  626  	if a.recoveryGate != nil {
  627  		a.observeRecoveryResult(ctx, evidenceName, evidenceArgs, readOnly, mutates, result, err, false, false, recoveryGen)
  628  	}
  629  	if err != nil {
  630  		detail := result
  631  		// Malformed-args failures are a transient model JSON glitch (e.g. options
  632  		// written as ["a":"b"] → "invalid character ':' after array element"). The
  633  		// args can't be safely re-parsed, but echoing the tool's schema makes the
  634  		// retry land valid instead of repeating the same broken shape.
  635  		if !json.Valid([]byte(call.Arguments)) {
  636  			detail = strings.TrimRight(detail, "\n") + "\nThe arguments were not valid JSON. Re-emit them exactly per this schema:\n" + string(t.Schema())
  637  		}
  638  		a.recordRepeatFailure(call, t, err)
  639  		body, truncMsg := truncateToolOutput(fmt.Sprintf("error: %v\n%s", err, detail))
  640  		return toolOutcome{output: body, errMsg: firstLine(err.Error()), truncated: truncMsg != "", truncMsg: truncMsg, recoveryGeneration: recoveryGen}
  641  	}
  642  	if mutates {
  643  		a.clearRepeatFailuresAfterMutation(evidenceName, evidenceArgs, readOnly)
  644  	}
  645  	a.recordRepeatSuccess(call, t)
  646  	// A foreground `task` sub-agent just finished — its result is the final answer.
  647  	// (A backgrounded one returns a "Started…" string and stops later in a job, so
  648  	// it doesn't fire here.) SubagentStop lets a hook react to delegated work.
  649  	if a.hooks != nil && call.Name == "task" && !isBackgroundTaskCall(call.Arguments) {
  650  		a.hooks.SubagentStop(ctx, result)
  651  	}
  652  	body, truncMsg := truncateToolOutput(result)
  653  	return toolOutcome{output: body, images: images, truncated: truncMsg != "", truncMsg: truncMsg, recoveryGeneration: recoveryGen}
  654  }

internal/agent/execute_one.go · 9aaf8d381a21当前源码 ↗
  552  func (a *Agent) prepareToolExecution(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) (toolOutcome, bool) {
  553  	policyArgs := plan.permArgs
  554  	if len(plan.resolved.Args) > 0 {
  555  		policyArgs = plan.resolved.Args
  556  	}
  557  	if outcome, blocked := a.taskPolicyToolGate(plan, policyArgs); blocked {
  558  		return outcome, true
  559  	}
  560  	// Acquire after permission is granted but before PreToolUse: hooks are user
  561  	// shell code and can themselves change the workspace. This keeps readers
  562  	// concurrent and avoids holding the workspace during an approval prompt while
  563  	// still covering every write-side action that follows authorization.
  564  	// Lazy workspace lease on the first real writer for every role setting.
  565  	if plan.effects.WorkspaceMutation && a.svc.workspaceLease != nil {
  566  		if err := a.svc.workspaceLease.AcquireWrite(ctx); err != nil {
  567  			return toolOutcome{
  568  				output:  fmt.Sprintf("blocked: the workspace did not become available for writing: %v", err),
  569  				blocked: true,
  570  				errMsg:  "blocked: workspace write lease unavailable",
  571  			}, true
  572  		}
  573  	}
  574  	// Resolve the concrete execution target before hooks. A proxy may carry a
  575  	// different target/name/argument set than the provider-visible call.
  576  	plan.runTool = plan.execTool
  577  	plan.runArgs = plan.execArgs
  578  	if plan.resolved.Target != nil {
  579  		plan.runTool = plan.resolved.Target
  580  		plan.runArgs = plan.resolved.Args
  581  		if len(plan.runArgs) == 0 {
  582  			plan.runArgs = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
  583  		}
  584  	}
  585  	// Hold the parent claim before PreToolUse: hooks are user shell code and may
  586  	// mutate the same workspace. The reservation remains live through hooks,
  587  	// checkpointing, and the concrete Execute call, closing both hook-side and
  588  	// check-before-write TOCTOU windows. Dynamic Economy/MCP tools are covered
  589  	// here after registry lookup without schema-changing wrappers.
  590  	// executeOne defers plan.releaseParentWrite so every return path releases.
  591  	if releaseParentWrite, perr := a.reserveParentWrite(plan.runTool, plan.runArgs, !plan.effects.WorkspaceMutation); perr != nil {
  592  		return toolOutcome{
  593  			output:  "blocked: " + perr.Error(),
  594  			blocked: true,
  595  			errMsg:  "blocked: write path claimed by background subagent",
  596  		}, true
  597  	} else if releaseParentWrite != nil {
  598  		plan.releaseParentWrite = releaseParentWrite
  599  	}
  600  	// Acquire the checkpoint barrier before preimage capture and any hook. It is
  601  	// held through post hooks and AfterMutation so rewind cannot interleave with
  602  	// writer-side user code.
  603  	if plan.effects.WorkspaceMutation && a.svc.mutationObserver != nil && a.svc.mutationObserver.Store() != nil {
  604  		barrier := a.svc.mutationObserver.Store().Barrier()
  605  		if err := barrier.EnterWrite(); err != nil {
  606  			return toolOutcome{output: "blocked: " + err.Error(), blocked: true, errMsg: "blocked: mutation barrier unavailable"}, true
  607  		}
  608  		plan.releaseMutationWrite = barrier.ExitWrite
  609  	}
  610  	// Checkpoint the file this writer is about to change before PreToolUse.
  611  	// A hook may mutate and then block the call, so the deferred AfterMutation
  612  	// still finalizes the fingerprint on every return path. Built-in
  613  	// Previewers get precise paths (complete coverage). Bash / opaque MCP
  614  	// writers record explicit coverage gaps instead of guessing targets.
  615  	if plan.effects.WorkspaceMutation {
  616  		a.observeBeforeMutation(ctx, plan)
  617  		plan.mutationObserved = plan.mutationPath != ""
  618  		if toolHooksMayMutateWorkspace(a.svc.hooks) && a.svc.mutationObserver != nil {
  619  			a.svc.mutationObserver.RecordGap(checkpoint.CoverageGap{Reason: checkpoint.GapHookWrite, Tool: plan.evidenceName, Detail: "tool hook may write paths that are not declared by the tool"})
  620  		}
  621  	}
  622  	// Proxy tools fire hooks against the real MCP target name and arguments.
  623  	if a.svc.hooks != nil {
  624  		if block, msg := a.svc.hooks.PreToolUse(ctx, plan.permName, plan.permArgs); block {
  625  			if msg == "" {
  626  				msg = "blocked by a PreToolUse hook"
  627  			}
  628  			return toolOutcome{
  629  				output:  "blocked: " + msg,
  630  				blocked: true,
  631  				errMsg:  "blocked by PreToolUse hook",
  632  			}, true
  633  		}
  634  	}
  635  	cctx := tool.WithContextCompressor(withCallContext(ctx, plan.call.ID, a.svc.sink, a.svc.asker, a.planMode.Load()), a)
  636  	cctx = WithSubagentDepth(cctx, a.subagentDepth)
  637  	if a.task.ledger != nil {
  638  		cctx = evidence.WithLedger(cctx, a.task.ledger)
  639  		cctx = evidence.WithSessionMessages(cctx, a.sess.conversation.Snapshot)
  640  		if a.deliveryProfile {
  641  			cctx = evidence.WithDeliveryProfile(cctx)
  642  		}
  643  	}
  644  	if !a.planMode.Load() {
  645  		cctx = a.withContractState(cctx)
  646  	}
  647  	if plan.planReplacementAuthorized {
  648  		cctx = tool.WithPlanReplacementAuthorization(cctx)
  649  	}
  650  	if len(a.projectChecks) > 0 {
  651  		cctx = instruction.WithChecks(cctx, a.projectChecks)
  652  	}
  653  	if a.svc.jobs != nil {
  654  		cctx = jobs.WithManager(cctx, a.svc.jobs)
  655  	}
  656  	if a.svc.sandboxEscape != nil {
  657  		cctx = sandbox.WithEscapeApprover(cctx, a.svc.sandboxEscape)
  658  	}
  659  	if a.svc.configWrite != nil {
  660  		cctx = tool.WithConfigWriteApprover(cctx, a.svc.configWrite)
  661  	}
  662  	if v := a.responseLanguage.Load(); v != nil {
  663  		if lang, ok := v.(string); ok {
  664  			cctx = WithResponseLanguagePreference(cctx, lang)
  665  		}
  666  	}
  667  	if v := a.reasoningLanguage.Load(); v != nil {
  668  		if lang, ok := v.(string); ok {
  669  			cctx = WithReasoningLanguagePreference(cctx, lang)
  670  		}
  671  	}
  672  	if a.svc.memQueue != nil {
  673  		cctx = memory.WithQueue(cctx, a.svc.memQueue)
  674  	}
  675  	callID := plan.call.ID
  676  	cctx = tool.WithProgress(cctx, func(chunk string) {
  677  		a.svc.sink.Emit(event.Event{Kind: event.ToolProgress, Tool: event.Tool{ID: callID, Output: chunk}})
  678  	})
  679  	plan.cctx = cctx
  680  	return toolOutcome{}, false
  681  }
  682
  683  type toolMutationHookReporter interface {
  684  	ToolMutationHooksEnabled() bool
  685  }
  686
  687  func toolHooksMayMutateWorkspace(hooks ToolHooks) bool {
  688  	if hooks == nil {
  689  		return false
  690  	}
  691  	if reporter, ok := hooks.(toolMutationHookReporter); ok {
  692  		return reporter.ToolMutationHooksEnabled()
  693  	}
  694  	// Custom ToolHooks implementations predate the capability report. Preserve
  695  	// conservative coverage for them because their callbacks may write files.
  696  	return true
  697  }
  698
  699  // finishToolExecution performs the concrete Execute, records evidence, runs
  700  // post hooks and recovery observation, and truncates the model-facing result.
  701  func (a *Agent) finishToolExecution(ctx context.Context, plan *toolCallPlan) toolOutcome {
  702  	plan.executed = true
  703  	cctx := plan.cctx
  704  	runTool := plan.runTool
  705  	runArgs := plan.runArgs
  706  	call := plan.call
  707  	t := plan.tool
  708  	readOnly := plan.readOnly
  709  	permName := plan.permName
  710  	permArgs := plan.permArgs
  711  	evidenceName := plan.evidenceName
  712  	evidenceArgs := plan.evidenceArgs
  713  	mutates := plan.effects.StateMutation
  714  	recoveryGen := plan.recoveryGen
  715
  716  	var result string
  717  	var images []string
  718  	var err error
  719  	// A call that was authorized under reader classification carries that
  720  	// basis into dispatch: the MCP execution layer re-verifies it linearizably
  721  	// against server authorization and live safety metadata, and refuses to
  722  	// promote it into a writer lane if reclassification landed after the gate.
  723  	if readOnly && isInstalledMCPTool(runTool) && mcpServerAuthorized(runTool) && !mcpDestructiveHint(runTool) {
  724  		cctx = tool.WithReaderExecutionIntent(cctx)
  725  	}
06
DIMENSION · CONNECTORS

连接器、插件与 MCP

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factconnectors

01 · MCP 以统一 JSON-RPC 适配 stdio、Streamable HTTP 和 legacy SSE

源码事实

plugin 包把握手、tools/list、tools/call 统一在 transport interface 下；stdio 用受 ctx 绑定的子进程与专用 reader demux，HTTP transport 用 session id、单 origin redirect 规则、16 MiB body cap 和串行 request/response mutex。

白话解释

外部工具可以是本地进程，也可以是远程 HTTP/SSE，但 Agent 看到的都是同一个 Tool；连接、超时、取消和响应大小在连接层处理。

对自研 Harness 的含义

连接器治理应是独立 runtime 层，不能让每个工具自己实现 JSON-RPC、超时和取消。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–3
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 4–7
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗

    1  // Package plugin is Reasonix's MCP client. It connects to external MCP servers and
    2  // adapts their tools to the tool.Tool interface, so the agent treats plugin
    3  // tools and built-ins uniformly. The wire protocol is JSON-RPC 2.0 in every
    4  // case; only the transport differs (stdio subprocess, Streamable HTTP, or the
    5  // legacy HTTP+SSE). A transport interface hides that difference so the MCP-level
    6  // logic — handshake, tools/list, tools/call — is written once.
    7  package plugin
    8

   10  	"context"

   34  // protocolVersion is the MCP revision Reasonix advertises during initialize.
   35  const protocolVersion = "2024-11-05"
   36
   37  // MCPProcessMode selects how a local stdio MCP process is launched.
   38  // It is an internal runtime field, not a user-facing config knob.
   39  type MCPProcessMode string
   40
   41  const (
   42  	// MCPProcessHost runs authorized stdio MCP as a trusted host process that
   43  	// does not inherit the agent Bash command sandbox. This is the product
   44  	// default so servers such as chrome-devtools-mcp can reach the real browser,
   45  	// Keychain, LaunchServices, and local app services.
   46  	MCPProcessHost MCPProcessMode = "host"
   47  	// MCPProcessConfined wraps the process with sandbox.CommandArgs. Reserved for
   48  	// internal managed deployments and tests; never auto-selected for user installs.
   49  	MCPProcessConfined MCPProcessMode = "confined"
   50  )
   51
   52  // ResolvedProcessMode returns the effective process mode. Empty means host.
   53  func (s Spec) ResolvedProcessMode() MCPProcessMode {
   54  	switch s.ProcessMode {
   55  	case MCPProcessConfined:
   56  		return MCPProcessConfined
   57  	default:
   58  		return MCPProcessHost
   59  	}
   60  }
   61
   62  // defaultCallTimeout is the MCP JSON-RPC call deadline applied when neither the
   63  // caller context nor config provides one. It is intentionally finite so a slow
   64  // or hung MCP server cannot block an agent turn indefinitely.
   65  const defaultCallTimeout = 300 * time.Second
   66
   67  // Spec declares an external MCP server. Type selects the transport: "stdio"
   68  // (default) runs Command/Args/Env as a subprocess; "http" / "streamable-http"
   69  // and "sse" connect to URL with optional static Headers.
   70  type Spec struct {
   71  	Name string
   72  	// Package is the installed plugin package that contributed this server.
   73  	// It is host-only provenance and intentionally excluded from fingerprints.
   74  	Package string
   75  	Type    string
   76  	Command string
   77  	Args    []string
   78  	Env     map[string]string

02
L1factconnectors

02 · MCP 项目服务器先做无 secret 的身份授权，再做 live safety 对账

源码事实

project launch identity 对 stdio 固定 executable path 与 SHA-256，对 HTTP 规范化 URL 但只保留 header 名并替换 userinfo/query credentials；cached tool 的 readOnly/destructive 事实与 live server 不一致时，当前调用在执行前阻断。MCP 默认 host mode 不进入 Bash sandbox，confined 只在显式内部模式使用。

白话解释

换了 MCP 二进制、目标地址或工具安全标记，不会因为旧 schema cache 还在就直接执行；同时默认插件是受信任 host 进程，不能误以为它自动继承 Agent shell 沙箱。

对自研 Harness 的含义

MCP 需要 provenance、exact launch grant、schema cache key 和 live reclassification 四件套；Host/Confined 必须在产品上清楚区分。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 17–40
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 41–66
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/plugin/security.go:17–66固定提交 ↗
   17  // projectLaunchIdentityDigest resolves a secret-free identity before a project
   18  // server is authorized. For stdio this pins the real executable path and file
   19  // content; for HTTP it normalizes the endpoint while retaining only header key
   20  // names. Installed and host-session servers never call this path.
   21  func projectLaunchIdentityDigest(ctx context.Context, s Spec) (string, error) {
   22  	identity, err := buildProjectLaunchIdentity(ctx, s)
   23  	if err != nil {
   24  		return "", err
   25  	}
   26  	return mcplaunch.ProjectLaunchIdentityDigest(identity)
   27  }
   28
   29  func buildProjectLaunchIdentity(ctx context.Context, s Spec) (mcplaunch.ProjectLaunchIdentity, error) {
   30  	transport := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(s.Type))
   31  	if transport == "" {
   32  		transport = "stdio"
   33  	}
   34  	launchArgs := effectiveLaunchArgs(s)
   35  	if s.LauncherIdentityArgs != nil {
   36  		launchArgs = s.LauncherIdentityArgs
   37  	}
   38  	identity := mcplaunch.ProjectLaunchIdentity{
   39  		Server: s.Name, Transport: transport,
   40  		Dir: s.Dir, Args: append([]string(nil), launchArgs...),
   41  		EnvKeys: sortedMapKeys(s.Env), HeaderKeys: sortedMapKeys(s.Headers),
   42  		LauncherDigest: s.LauncherDigest,
   43  	}
   44  	switch transport {
   45  	case "stdio":
   46  		identity.Dir = stdioWorkingDir(s)
   47  		if strings.TrimSpace(s.Command) == "" {
   48  			return mcplaunch.ProjectLaunchIdentity{}, fmt.Errorf("stdio plugin %q: command is required", s.Name)
   49  		}
   50  		env := mergeEnv(secrets.ProcessEnv(), s.Env)
   51  		exe, _, err := resolveStdioExecutable(ctx, s, env)
   52  		if err != nil {
   53  			return mcplaunch.ProjectLaunchIdentity{}, err
   54  		}
   55  		identity.CommandPath = exe
   56  		identity.CommandSHA256, err = mcplaunch.FileSHA256(exe)
   57  		if err != nil {
   58  			return mcplaunch.ProjectLaunchIdentity{}, fmt.Errorf("hash MCP executable %q: %w", exe, err)
   59  		}
   60  	case "http", "streamable-http", "streamable_http":
   61  		identity.Transport = "http"
   62  		identity.URL = normalizeIdentityURL(s.URL)
   63  	default:
   64  		identity.URL = normalizeIdentityURL(s.URL)
   65  	}
   66  	return identity, nil
   67  }
   68
   69  // MCPStateDir returns a stable, server-scoped host directory outside the
   70  // workspace for state that must survive across calls and sessions.
   71  func MCPStateDir(reasonixHome, workspace, server string) string {
   72  	if strings.TrimSpace(reasonixHome) == "" {
   73  		return ""
   74  	}
   75  	workspaceID := mcplaunch.WorkspaceFingerprint(workspace)
   76  	if len(workspaceID) > 16 {
   77  		workspaceID = workspaceID[:16]
   78  	}
   79  	if workspaceID == "" {
   80  		workspaceID = "global"
   81  	}
   82  	return filepath.Join(reasonixHome, "mcp-state", workspaceID, slug(server))
   83  }
   84
   85  // identityURLRedacted replaces credential material inside identity and cache
   86  // URLs. Only the structure survives: whether userinfo/a password exists and
   87  // how many values a credential parameter carries, never their contents.
   88  const identityURLRedacted = "__redacted__"
   89
   90  // credentialURLQueryKeys lists query parameters whose values are credentials.
   91  // Keys are compared case-insensitively after removing "-" and "_", so
   92  // api_key, api-key, x-api-key, and APIKEY normalize consistently, and any
   93  // normalized key ending in a credentialURLQuerySuffixes entry (auth_token,
   94  // refresh_token, id_token, client_secret, sas_signature, ...) is a credential
   95  // too. Non-sensitive parameters (workspace, tenant, region, resource, ...)
   96  // keep their values so a resource scope change still re-triggers verification.
   97  var credentialURLQueryKeys = map[string]bool{
   98  	"auth": true, "authorization": true, "bearer": true, "credential": true,
   99  	"credentials": true, "sig": true,
  100  	// The key family stays an exact list: a bare "*key" suffix would also
  101  	// swallow unrelated words (monkey, sortkey-like resource names).
  102  	"key": true, "accesskey": true, "secretkey": true, "privatekey": true,
  103  	"authkey": true, "appkey": true, "clientkey": true, "subscriptionkey": true,
  104  	"sharedkey": true,
  105  }
  106
  107  // credentialURLQuerySuffixes classifies whole credential families by suffix:
  108  // every *token, *secret, *password/*passwd, *apikey, and *signature parameter
  109  // carries a credential value regardless of its prefix.
  110  var credentialURLQuerySuffixes = []string{
  111  	"token", "secret", "password", "passwd", "apikey", "signature",
  112  }
  113
  114  func credentialURLQueryKey(key string) bool {
  115  	normalized := strings.NewReplacer("-", "", "_", "").Replace(strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(key)))
  116  	if credentialURLQueryKeys[normalized] {
  117  		return true
  118  	}
  119  	for _, suffix := range credentialURLQuerySuffixes {
  120  		if strings.HasSuffix(normalized, suffix) {
  121  			return true
  122  		}
  123  	}
  124  	return false
  125  }
  126
  127  // normalizeIdentityURL canonicalizes an MCP endpoint for host-local identity
  128  // and schema-cache keys: scheme/host case and default ports fold,
  129  // the fragment drops, query keys sort stably, and credential material
  130  // (userinfo, credential query values) is replaced by a fixed placeholder so
  131  // rotation never invalidates an exact project launch authorization.
  132  // Network requests always use the raw configured URL, never this form.
  133  func normalizeIdentityURL(raw string) string {
  134  	u, err := url.Parse(strings.TrimSpace(raw))
  135  	if err != nil || u.Scheme == "" || u.Host == "" {
  136  		return strings.TrimSpace(raw)
  137  	}

03
L1factconnectors

03 · MCP 启动是 catalog-first、cache-aware、可惰性连接的

源码事实

boot 对有缓存 schema 的服务器只注册 placeholder，不启动进程；cache miss 才做一次后台 catalog discovery，真正首次调用走 EnsureConnected。插件启动有默认并发上限 8、超时和 StartAvailable 的“单个失败不拖垮整场”路径；Economy profile 还把可选 MCP 放到 connect_tool_source。

白话解释

开一个项目不会立刻 fork 二十个 npm MCP 进程；先用缓存把工具菜单画出来，真正用到时再连，坏一个插件也不必让整场会话起不来。

对自研 Harness 的含义

扩展生态的启动成本应被当成调度问题，采用 schema cache、lazy process 和 bounded startup，而不是启动时全量阻塞。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 243–260
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 261–279
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  243  // StartPolicy tunes batch plugin startup. The zero value disables every safeguard,
  244  // so most call sites should use the StartAll / StartAvailable wrappers, which
  245  // fill in production defaults.
  246  type StartPolicy struct {
  247  	// PerPluginTimeout caps how long a single plugin's handshake (start +
  248  	// initialize + listTools + listPrompts/Resources) may take. Zero disables.
  249  	// Exceeded plugins are recorded as failures and, when AbortOnError is set,
  250  	// tear down the whole batch with the timeout as the cause.
  251  	PerPluginTimeout time.Duration
  252
  253  	// Concurrency caps how many handshakes run at once. Zero or negative means
  254  	// no cap (every plugin gets a goroutine immediately). A small cap prevents
  255  	// process storms / FD exhaustion when many MCP servers are configured.
  256  	Concurrency int
  257
  258  	// AbortOnError makes any single failure tear down the partial batch and
  259  	// return an error (StartAll semantics). When false, failures are recorded
  260  	// on the host and other plugins keep going (StartAvailable semantics).
  261  	AbortOnError bool
  262
  263  	// SkipPersistence disables RecordStartup / SaveCachedSchema side effects.
  264  	// Use for read-only live probes (capability diagnostics) that must not
  265  	// write MCP stats or schema cache files under Reasonix home.
  266  	SkipPersistence bool
  267  }
  268
  269  // defaultStartConcurrency caps parallel handshakes for the batch-start wrappers.
  270  // Eight is the standard "process storm" guardrail (Bazel's --jobs=auto, most LSP
  271  // managers) — large enough to mask single-plugin latency, small enough to spare
  272  // a workstation with 20+ configured MCP servers from fork-bombing itself.
  273  const defaultStartConcurrency = 8
  274
  275  // defaultStartTimeout is the per-plugin budget used by StartAvailable. Five
  276  // seconds covers a healthy stdio MCP spawning under a slow npm/node loader; past
  277  // that, an interactive user is better served by recording the failure and moving
  278  // on than by stalling the whole session.
  279  const defaultStartTimeout = 5 * time.Second
  280
  281  var advertisedToolsEmptyListRetryDelays = []time.Duration{
  282  	50 * time.Millisecond,
  283  	150 * time.Millisecond,
  284  	300 * time.Millisecond,
  285  }

  288  // is already live on the host.
  289  var ErrServerAlreadyConnected = errors.New("plugin server already connected")
  290
  291  func serverAlreadyConnectedError(name string) error {
  292  	return fmt.Errorf("%w: %q", ErrServerAlreadyConnected, name)
  293  }
  294
  295  // IsServerAlreadyConnected reports whether err means the MCP server name is
  296  // already live on the host.
  297  func IsServerAlreadyConnected(err error) bool {
  298  	return errors.Is(err, ErrServerAlreadyConnected)
  299  }

  301  // StartAll connects every plugin in parallel, performs the MCP handshake, and
  302  // returns the union of their tools (namespaced "mcp__<server>__<tool>"). On any
  303  // failure it tears down everything started so far. The caller must Close the Host.
  304  //
  305  // For stdio plugins, subprocess lifetime is bound to ctx (via
  306  // exec.CommandContext): cancelling ctx kills the children and unblocks reads.
  307  func StartAll(ctx context.Context, specs []Spec) (*Host, []tool.Tool, error) {
  308  	return Start(ctx, specs, StartPolicy{
  309  		Concurrency:  defaultStartConcurrency,
  310  		AbortOnError: true,
  311  	})
  312  }
  313
  314  // StartAvailable connects every plugin it can and records failures on the host
  315  // instead of aborting the whole session. The returned tools are the union of the
  316  // successfully connected servers.
  317  func StartAvailable(ctx context.Context, specs []Spec) (*Host, []tool.Tool) {
  318  	h, tools, _ := Start(ctx, specs, StartPolicy{
  319  		PerPluginTimeout: defaultStartTimeout,
  320  		Concurrency:      defaultStartConcurrency,
  321  		// AbortOnError stays false: a misconfigured plugin must not bring down
  322  		// the whole session at boot.
  323  	})
  324  	return h, tools
  325  }
  326
  327  // Start is the unified batch-startup primitive behind StartAll / StartAvailable.
  328  // It fans out handshakes in parallel under the policy's concurrency cap, gives
  329  // each plugin its own per-plugin timeout, and either aborts the batch on first
  330  // failure (AbortOnError=true) or records failures on the host and keeps going.
  331  //
  332  // Result ordering matches specs (stable for /mcp status). For stdio plugins the
  333  // subprocess is bound to the parent ctx, not the per-plugin startup timeout:
  334  // successful servers stay alive after startup, while failed/time-limited starts
  335  // are closed explicitly before the goroutine returns.
  336  func Start(ctx context.Context, specs []Spec, p StartPolicy) (*Host, []tool.Tool, error) {
  337  	if len(specs) == 0 {
  338  		return &Host{}, nil, nil
  339  	}
  340
  341  	type result struct {
  342  		idx    int
  343  		spec   Spec
  344  		client *Client
  345  		tools  []tool.Tool
  346  		err    error
  347  	}
  348

  350  	// means no cap — we still launch one goroutine per spec, but they all run

  312  // Start is the unified batch-startup primitive behind StartAll / StartAvailable.
  313  // It fans out handshakes in parallel under the policy's concurrency cap, gives
  314  // each plugin its own per-plugin timeout, and either aborts the batch on first
  315  // failure (AbortOnError=true) or records failures on the host and keeps going.
  316  //
  317  // Result ordering matches specs (stable for /mcp status). For stdio plugins the
  318  // subprocess is bound to the parent ctx, not the per-plugin startup timeout:
  319  // successful servers stay alive after startup, while failed/time-limited starts
  320  // are closed explicitly before the goroutine returns.
  321  func Start(ctx context.Context, specs []Spec, p StartPolicy) (*Host, []tool.Tool, error) {
  322  	if len(specs) == 0 {
  323  		return &Host{}, nil, nil
  324  	}
  325
  326  	type result struct {
  327  		idx    int
  328  		spec   Spec
  329  		client *Client
  330  		tools  []tool.Tool
  331  		err    error
  332  	}
  333
  334  	// A buffered channel acts as a counting semaphore. Capacity 0/negative
  335  	// means no cap — we still launch one goroutine per spec, but they all run
  336  	// immediately. Capped, the extra goroutines block on the semaphore until a
  337  	// slot frees up; collection order is still by idx so /mcp status is stable.
  338  	concurrency := p.Concurrency
  339  	if concurrency <= 0 || concurrency > len(specs) {
  340  		concurrency = len(specs)
  341  	}
  342  	sem := make(chan struct{}, concurrency)
  343  	ch := make(chan result, len(specs))
  344
  345  	// Created before the fan-out so the detached cache writers can join bgWrites.
  346  	h := &Host{}
  347
  348  	for i, s := range specs {
  349  		go func(idx int, spec Spec) {
  350  			sem <- struct{}{}
07
DIMENSION · SECURITY

安全、审批与权限

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factsecurity

01 · 权限 Policy 是纯函数，deny→ask→allow→fallback 且按每个路径判定

源码事实

permission.Policy 不做 I/O；read-only 默认 Allow，writer 使用 Mode fallback；Decision 优先级为 deny、session allow、ask、allow、fallback。多路径工具必须所有 subject 都安全，Bash 还会把 compound command 拆段，识别 eval/source/xargs/解释器 -c 等间接执行形状。

白话解释

权限规则可以单测；一个 move 同时碰两个路径时，只要一个路径被拒就拒绝整单；`bash -c`、`eval` 这类能藏第二条命令的写法不会被普通前缀规则轻易放过。

对自研 Harness 的含义

把 shell parser 和 policy decision 分离是比字符串黑名单更稳的权限基础，但仍需要持续覆盖新 shell 语法。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–2
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 3–5
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 22e18cafc0df · 打开账本提交 ↗
internal/permission/permission.go:1–5固定提交 ↗
    1  // Package permission decides, per tool call, whether to allow it, deny it, or
    2  // ask the user first. The core is a pure Policy (rule evaluation, no I/O); a
    3  // Gate wraps a Policy with an optional interactive Approver and is what the
    4  // agent consults at execute time. Keeping rule evaluation pure makes it
    5  // trivially testable and keeps the agent independent of how "ask" is resolved.
    6  package permission
    7
    8  import (
    9  	"context"
   10  	"encoding/json"
   11  	"fmt"
   12  	"strings"
   13
   14  	"reasonix/internal/shellparse"
   15  )
   16
   17  // Decision is the outcome of evaluating a tool call against a Policy.
   18  type Decision int
   19
   20  const (
   21  	// Allow runs the tool without prompting.
   22  	Allow Decision = iota
   23  	// Ask defers to an interactive Approver (or, with none, resolves to Allow).
   24  	Ask
   25  	// Deny blocks the tool in every mode.
   26  	Deny
   27  )
   28
   29  func (d Decision) String() string {
   30  	switch d {
   31  	case Allow:
   32  		return "allow"
   33  	case Ask:
   34  		return "ask"
   35  	case Deny:
   36  		return "deny"
   37  	default:
   38  		return "unknown"
   39  	}
   40  }
   41
   42  // ParseDecision maps a config string to a Decision. Unknown / empty input
   43  // defaults to Ask — the conservative posture for a writer fallback.
   44  func ParseDecision(s string) Decision {
   45  	switch strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(s)) {
   46  	case "allow":
   47  		return Allow
   48  	case "deny":
   49  		return Deny
   50  	default:
   51  		return Ask
   52  	}
   53  }
   54
   55  // Rule matches tool calls. Tool is the tool name; Subject, when non-empty,
   56  // constrains the call's subject. A glob Subject (see matchGlob) matches by
   57  // wildcard; a Literal Subject matches by exact string equality. An empty Subject
   58  // matches every call to Tool.
   59  type Rule struct {
   60  	Tool    string
   61  	Subject string
   62  	// Literal matches Subject by exact equality rather than as a glob, so a
   63  	// remembered concrete command keeps any '*'/'?' as ordinary characters
   64  	// instead of turning them into wildcards.
   65  	Literal bool
   66  }
   67
   68  // ParseRule parses "ToolName", "ToolName(glob)", or the legacy
   69  // "ToolName=literal" form. Surrounding whitespace is trimmed. The "=literal"
   70  // form (taken when the '=' precedes any '(') matches the rest of the string
   71  // verbatim — no globbing — and is kept for existing configs that were written
   72  // before the Claude Code-style Tool(specifier) approval rules. ok is false for
   73  // a malformed entry (empty tool name) so the caller can warn rather than
   74  // silently install a rule that matches nothing.
   75  func ParseRule(s string) (Rule, bool) {
   76  	s = strings.TrimSpace(s)

02
L1factsecurity

02 · 凭据防护同时覆盖子进程环境、敏感文件和诊断文本

源码事实

secrets 由 boot 从 user-global 配置一次性设置；ProcessEnv 永远移除 credential-store 注入的 key，用户可选择再过滤继承环境中的 api_key/token/password 等变量；Redact/RedactCredentials 覆盖 bearer、OpenAI/GitHub/Slack/AWS/JWT、Cookie 和 URL userinfo。

白话解释

项目目录里的配置不能偷偷改全局 secret 开关；工具子进程拿到的是清理后的环境，日志/错误边界还会把常见 token 形状打码。

对自研 Harness 的含义

凭据过滤必须在 composition root 和 subprocess boundary 做，而不是只在最终 UI 文本上补救。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 200–204
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 205–211
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。
